### [1] [Temporal and Conceptual Modeling: Foundations and Research Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05342)
*Nina Edelweiss,José Palazzo M. de Oliveira*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文回顾了Nina Edelweiss和José Palazzo Moreira de Oliveira在1993至1997年间开展的时态与概念建模研究，并追踪其在UFRGS指导的研究生工作中产生的影响，识别出从形式化时态数据库模型到动态与基于知识的信息系统的连贯演进脉络，这些贡献预见了当代在溯源、本体演化、知识图谱等领域的研究关注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统地梳理和总结Nina Edelweiss与José Palazzo Moreira de Oliveira在时态与概念建模领域的研究发展脉络及其后续影响力，揭示其研究成果如何通过研究生培养形成连贯的学术传承，并与当代信息系统研究前沿建立联系。

Method: 采用文献回顾与学术谱系分析的方法，首先追踪1993至1997年间一系列扩展面向对象建模的研究（包括时态数据类型、历史状态、双时态查询、多模型集成、角色与模式演化），然后分析在UFRGS指导下开展的研究生工作，通过连接TF-ORM框架与后续研究轨迹来识别研究发展的内在逻辑。

Result: 论文识别出一条从形式化时态数据库模型到动态及基于知识的信息系统的连贯发展路径，涉及时态查询处理、索引、版本管理、模式版本化、自适应系统、信息集成和演化知识等多个研究方向，并论证了这些早期工作对当代溯源、可追踪性、本体演化、知识图谱和语义一致自适应系统的预见性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，早期时态与概念建模研究不仅构成了形式化数据库理论的基础，其学术传承还延伸到当代信息系统研究的关键议题，具有重要的历史价值和前瞻性意义。

Abstract: This paper examines the development and subsequent influence of temporal and conceptual modeling research conducted by Nina Edelweiss and José Palazzo Moreira de Oliveira. It traces a sequence of studies from 1993 to 1997 that extended object-oriented modeling with temporal data types, historical states, bitemporal queries, multi-model integration, roles, and schema evolution. The analysis then considers graduate research supervised at UFRGS, showing how these foundations supported work on temporal query processing, indexing, version management, schema versioning, adaptive systems, information integration, and evolving knowledge. By connecting the original TF-ORM framework to subsequent research trajectories, the paper identifies a coherent progression from formal temporal database models to broader approaches to dynamic and knowledge-based information systems. These contributions anticipated current concerns in provenance, traceability, ontology evolution, knowledge graphs, and semantically consistent adaptive systems.

</details>


### [2] [BEGIN AI TRANSACTION: Semantic Isolation for Durable AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05412)
*Barzan Mozafari*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出AI工作流中的"语义隔离"问题：当检查点保存的状态与运行时变化的语义环境（如提示词、模型别名、工具）不一致时，会产生内部不一致的结果。作者定义了四种可自动检测的异常，推导出三个独立保证组合成的隔离级别偏序，并在LangGraph代码审计中发现7.4%的代码库存在此类风险，原型SemIso以微秒级检查实现高效防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI执行已从单次模型调用演变为跨暂停、重试、子代理和工具选择的多阶段工作流。然而，工作流检查点机制缺乏类似数据库事务对并发数据更新的隔离契约——语义环境（提示词、模型、工具等）可能在工作流执行期间被独立变更，导致保存的状态与改变的假设不兼容，产生内部不一致的结果。

Method: 定义了四种可自动检测的异常（语义读偏斜、兼容性偏斜、上下文逃逸、合并偏斜）；推导出从语义读已提交到语义快照隔离的隔离级别偏序，由资源稳定性、跨资源兼容性和延续继承三个独立保证组合而成；通过中间件原型SemIso实现语义上下文的传播与不兼容资源的阻断检查。

Result: 对100个最受欢迎的包含可执行LangGraph代码的公共仓库进行保守源码审计，发现7.4%拥有持久化工作流的代码库在同一工作流内解析活体或动态选择的语义资源，且缺乏不可变绑定。SemIso原型以微秒级开销完成检查与强制。

Conclusion: AI工作流需要一个类似数据库事务隔离级别的语义隔离概念框架，本文提供了检测异常、定义隔离级别的理论基础，并证明了其在中间件层面可被高效实现。

Abstract: An AI execution can now outlive the environment in which it began. What once fit inside one model call increasingly unfolds across pauses, retries, branches, subagents, and model-selected tools. Meanwhile, prompts, model aliases, indexes, policies, and tools are deployed independently: stable names can acquire new behavior, and workflows can discover resources only after they start. The workflow can therefore combine saved state with changed assumptions, producing an internally inconsistent result even when every call succeeds. This is an isolation problem: database transactions constrain concurrent data updates, but workflow checkpointing provides no corresponding contract for concurrent changes to an AI workflow's semantic environment.
  We define four automatically detectable anomalies: semantic read skew, compatibility skew, context escape, and merge skew. To control which anomalies are allowed, we derive a partial order of isolation levels, from Semantic Read Committed to Semantic Snapshot Isolation, by combining three independent guarantees: resource stability, cross-resource compatibility, and continuation inheritance. In a conservative source audit of the 100 most-starred public repositories with executable LangGraph code, we find that 7.4 percent of codebases with durable workflows resolve live or dynamically selected semantic resources within the same workflow, without an evident immutable binding. We show that these guarantees can be checked and enforced efficiently in middleware. Our prototype, SemIso, propagates semantic context and blocks incompatible resources and branch merges with microsecond-scale checks.

</details>


### [3] [Filtered Vector Search in a Disaggregated Lakehouse: Composing Table-Format Pruning with Per-File ANN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05441)
*Rakesh Jain,Thomas Griffin,Syed Zawad*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文探讨了如何在开放湖仓表（Apache Iceberg + Parquet）中原生集成近似最近邻（ANN）搜索，将IVF索引嵌入每个Parquet文件footer，通过组合表已有的文件剪枝机制（分区剪枝、zone-map、位图索引）与逐文件ANN搜索来实现快速过滤向量查询，而非设计新的过滤算法。在11.5M×768数据集上，暖IVF搜索比暴力检索快约32倍（recall@10≥0.90），在500万IBM Granite嵌入上实现约94倍加速，并系统分析了该组合策略的适用条件与失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ANN搜索与结构化过滤通常分离实现（专用向量索引+独立过滤步骤），而实际场景中两者常同时出现（如"找最近邻文档且满足某tenant和lang条件"）。作者希望探究当两者同时存在于开放湖仓表内时，能否利用引擎已有的成熟文件剪枝栈来提升过滤向量查询的性能，从而避免维护两套独立系统的复杂性。

Method: 将IVF索引内嵌到每个Parquet文件的footer中，构建分布式且非破坏性的索引（仅元数据替换的Iceberg replace操作）。查询时由规划器先用谓词剪枝数据文件，再仅对幸存文件运行IVF搜索。采用一致性哈希的逐文件缓存来避免对象存储读取延迟抵消算法收益。同时分析了组合策略的适用条件：过滤列需要文件级局部性，且残留谓词只在纯（分区）列上可安全下推，而非仅排序的列。

Result: 在11.5M×768的表上，暖IVF搜索比暴力检索快约32倍（recall@10≥0.90），选择性谓词在ANN运行前剪掉了444个数据文件中的355个；在500万真实IBM Granite嵌入上，join产生的过滤剪掉了5个区域分区中的4个，将14.7秒降至157毫秒（约94倍加速），且与查询时join在相同top-k下结果一致。

Conclusion: 组合式方案（文件剪枝+逐文件ANN）的收益完全来自文件剪枝，在具备文件级局部性的过滤列上效果显著。但该方法并非普适——需要过滤列满足文件级局部性，且残留谓词下推仅在纯分区列上安全。作者还报告了将ANN接入湖仓引擎时遇到的各类失败模式，为后续实践提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Approximate nearest-neighbor (ANN) search increasingly runs alongside structured data - "find the 10 nearest documents where tenant='acme' AND lang='en'" - yet similarity and filtering are usually bolted together: a specialized vector index for one, a separate filter step for the other. We ask what happens when both live inside an open lakehouse table (Apache Iceberg over Parquet on object storage), where the engine already owns a mature file-pruning stack (partition pruning, zone-maps, a bitmap index). We embed an IVF index in place in each Parquet file's footer and make filtered vector queries fast not with a new filtering algorithm but by composing the table's existing file pruning with per-file ANN: the planner prunes data files by the predicate first, then runs IVF only over the survivors. The index is built distributed and non-destructively - a metadata-only Iceberg replace that every other engine still reads - and a rendezvous-hashed per-file cache keeps object-store read latency from swamping the algorithmic win. The payoff comes entirely from file pruning. On an 11.5M x 768 table, warm IVF search is ~32x faster than brute force at recall@10 >= 0.90, a selective predicate having pruned 355 of 444 data files before ANN runs; on 5M real IBM Granite embeddings, a filter arriving across a join prunes four of five region partitions and runs nearly two orders of magnitude (~94x: 14.7 s -> 157 ms) faster than the query-time join at identical top-k, once the reduction is materialized into a region-partitioned layout. We characterize when the composition pays off - it requires file-level locality on the filter column, and the residual predicate is only safe to push into the search over a provably pure (partitioned) column, not a merely sorted one - and report the failure modes we hit bolting ANN onto a lakehouse engine.

</details>


### [4] [Window Function Optimization: Co-Evaluation and Other Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06043)
*Daniel Lindner,Felix Naumann,Alberto Lerner*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了窗口函数优化的系统框架，包括帧分析（Frame Analysis）、分区分析（Partition Analysis）和一种新的执行策略——协同评估（Co-Evaluation），通过代数等价变换实现窗口函数的谓词下推等优化。在开源引擎中测试表明优化从不损害性能，某些常见查询提速最高达40.7倍，且表越大收益越大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 窗口函数是现代SQL中最具表现力的特性之一，但针对其优化的研究相对匮乏。现有优化技术（如谓词下推）仅在非常理想的条件下适用，一旦条件稍有偏离便失效。作者认为这些局限并非根本性的，而是因为缺少一个用于窗口函数优化的推理框架。

Method: 本文提出三组关键技术：帧分析（Frame Analysis）用于分析窗口帧的边界与依赖关系；分区分析（Partition Analysis）用于分析分区键的约束；协同评估（Co-Evaluation）是一种新的执行策略，允许即使谓词依赖窗口函数结果也能提前求值。这些方法以代数等价性的表格形式系统组织呈现。

Result: 在开源SQL引擎中实现并测试了这些优化技术。结果表明优化不会损害性能，对于某些常见查询可实现最高40.7倍的加速，并且数据表越大，性能提升越显著。

Conclusion: 窗口函数优化的先前局限并非本质性的，通过引入系统的推理框架（帧分析、分区分析和协同评估）可以显著扩展窗口函数可优化的范围，实现大幅性能提升。

Abstract: Window functions are among the most expressive features of modern SQL. Surprisingly, relatively little has been written about their optimization. Some techniques exist, such as pushing predicates through a window under ideal conditions, but known optimizations no longer apply when those conditions are even slightly unmet. We show that these limitations are not fundamental, but persist because a reasoning framework for window function optimization has been missing. We provide such a framework, introducing techniques we call Frame Analysis, Partition Analysis, and a new execution strategy called Co-Evaluation. These clarify when and how optimizations can be applied. Co-Evaluation, in particular, allows early evaluation of predicates even when they depend on the window function's result. We present each technique and organize the results as a table of algebraic equivalences for window functions. We test these optimizations in an open-source engine, where they never hurt performance and make certain common queries up to 40.7 times faster, with larger tables yielding larger gains.

</details>


### [5] [PLB: Priority-Aware Load Balancing for Replicated Databases under Constrained Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06140)
*Belkis Djeffal,Pierre Bourhis,Romain Rouvoy*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出PLB，一个基于JDBC驱动的优先级感知负载均衡器，通过控制客户端会话在数据库副本之间的分配来实现优先级区分。PLB将副本按用户组（高端与免费）分区，并采用基于负载的借用机制，在固定资源条件下，既保证高优先级用户使用空闲容量，又控制低优先级用户的性能降级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据库系统通常对所有会话一视同仁，但在固定容量（无法按需添加副本）且工作负载出现争用的情况下，会产生资源过度配置与低优先级用户大幅减速之间的艰难权衡。需要一种在固定资源下实现优先级区分的机制。

Method: PLB通过JDBC驱动程序实现，作为优先级感知的负载均衡器，将副本按用户组（premium与freemium）分区，并采用基于负载的借用机制：高优先级用户可在空闲时借用低优先级副本容量，同时控制低优先级用户的性能降级。

Result: 在复制型只读OLAP集群上评估：相比静态专用优先级分区，PLB在固定分区可将集群CPU利用率降至约35%的场景下，将利用率保持在约70%以上，同时延迟接近最佳专用分配。相比完全共享的轮询池，PLB将高优先级中位延迟平均降低约12%（最高达28%），而低优先级中位开销保持在约11%，总体中位延迟接近轮询方式。

Conclusion: PLB通过副本分配实现优先级区分，在固定资源场景下有效平衡了资源利用率和优先级性能保障，为数据库系统中的优先级差异化服务提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Priority-differentiated services are a standard way for applications to offer different levels of performance, but database systems still often treat all sessions the same way. When database capacity is fixed, meaning replicas cannot be added on demand, and the workload becomes contended, this creates a difficult trade-off between over-provisioning resources and letting lower-priority users experience much larger slowdowns. In such settings, we propose enforcing priority by controlling how client sessions are assigned to database replicas.
  We present PLB, a priority-aware load balancer implemented as a JDBC driver that enforces priority differentiation through replica assignment under fixed resources. PLB partitions replicas by user group, premium versus freemium, and uses load-based borrowing so that higher-priority users can use idle capacity when available, while degradation for lower-priority users remains controlled. We evaluate PLB on a replicated read-only cluster under OLAP workloads. Compared with static dedicated per-priority partitions, PLB keeps utilization above about 70% in settings where fixed partitions can reduce cluster-wide CPU utilization to about 35%, while maintaining latencies close to those of the best dedicated allocation. Compared with a fully shared round-robin pool, PLB lowers high-priority median latency by about 12% on average, with improvements of up to 28%, while keeping the low-priority median overhead around 11% and overall median latency close to round-robin.

</details>


### [6] [Tytan: Interactive Neurosymbolic Construction of Analytic Semantic Schemas from Relational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06331)
*Donna Hooshmand,Shubham Shahi,Cameron Barrie,Abhratanu Dutta,Marko Sterbentz,Harper Pack,Kristian J. Hammond*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: TYTAN是一个自动从关系数据库构建分析性语义模式的系统，结合符号分析与LLM语义推理，在八个数据库上实现了100%的实体覆盖率和检索正确率，语义角色准确率达92-100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据语义层通常需要人工编写，这是知识获取的瓶颈，限制了分析系统的可扩展性，使非技术用户依赖专家，且本身容易出错。需要一个自动构建语义模式的系统来解决这一问题。

Method: TYTAN结合了数据库的符号分析与基于LLM的语义推理，用于实体提议、角色分配和命名。当证据不足以做出决定时，系统会向用户提出有针对性的自然语言问题来消除歧义。

Result: 在七个参考域上实现100%覆盖率（所有实体、属性和可聚合特征），100%的检索指令正确执行（1678/1678），语义角色一致率92-100%（小差异经核实来自参考而非TYTAN）。在盲测中，恢复了完整的实体结构并满足100%的可满足预期。

Conclusion: TYTAN能够自动构建高质量的分析性语义模式，克服了人工编写语义层的瓶颈，在覆盖率、检索正确性和特征准确性三个维度上均表现出优异性能。

Abstract: From natural-language query interfaces to automated report generation, data analysis tools need a description of the data: the real-world entities it contains, which columns function as measures or identifiers, and how tables connect into units of analysis. Today, this semantic layer is usually written by hand. This is a knowledge-acquisition bottleneck that limits the scalability of analytic systems, keeps non-technical users dependent on experts, and is itself error-prone. We present TYTAN, a system for automatically constructing an analytic semantic schema from a relational database and, when available, a short user-provided description. TYTAN combines symbolic analysis of the database with LLM-based semantic inference for entity proposal, role assignment, and naming. When the evidence leaves a decision ambiguous, TYTAN asks the user a targeted natural-language question. We evaluate TYTAN on eight databases spanning real-world and benchmark domains along the three axes that define a schema's functional utility: (i) coverage, are all important entities and features captured?; (ii) retrieval correctness, do the schema's instructions actually reach the data; and (iii) characterization accuracy, are semantic types correct? Across the seven reference domains, TYTAN reaches every entity, attribute, and aggregable feature of the expert-corrected reference schemas (100% coverage). Additionally, 100% of its retrieval instructions execute correctly (1,678 of 1,678 self-generated claims), and semantic roles agree with the reference on 92-100% of matched attributes. Checking the underlying data showed the small disagreement is in the reference, not in TYTAN. On a held-out blind test (a live, ten-table database with no declared keys), TYTAN recovers the full entity structure with verified keys and satisfies 100% of the satisfiable expectations of five independent blind annotators.

</details>

### [7] [Towards a Risk Assessment of Malicious Skill Files in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05223)
*Rui Yang,Michael Fu,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Chetan Arora,Joey Chua*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了自主编码代理中的"代理技能接口"安全漏洞，提出了一种对抗性技能合成方法，通过六个LLM家族将471个真实shell命令转化为2,826个看似良性的恶意技能，并揭示了Gemini CLI和Qwen Code在95.5-96.1%和71.6-74.0%的运行中被利用的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码代理越来越多地嵌入企业软件工作流中并被赋予对连接系统的委派权限，而代理技能接口（用于动态加载指令和脚本的文件夹）扩大了攻击面，使恶意shell命令可以隐藏在看似无害的自然语言技能文件中，需要系统性地评估这一安全风险。

Method: 提出三方面工作：1) 利用六种LLM跨越四个家族将471个真实shell命令转化为看似良性的对抗性技能，生成包含2,826个技能的数据集并映射到11个MITRE ATT&CK战术；2) 构建可复现的评估流水线，结合运行分层、证据锚定、拒绝否决和确定性声明意图覆盖，以及三人LLM评审团，并用盲人人类金标准验证（Cohen's kappa=0.85）；3) 对两个企业级代理进行5,629次完整运行的大规模特征化评估。

Result: Gemini CLI在95.5-96.1%的运行中被利用，Qwen Code在71.6-74.0%的运行中被利用（从原始多数投票到声明意图修正估计，均在人类金标准范围内），且结果几乎不受生成模型影响。明确的安全识别仅在1.99%的运行中出现。

Conclusion: 企业在采用编码代理之前必须评估并缓解技能接口风险，现有编码代理对恶意技能注入的抵御能力严重不足。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents are increasingly embedded in enterprise software workflows with delegated authority over connected systems. Central to this architecture is the agent skills interface: folders of instructions and scripts that agents load dynamically to specialize their behavior. This interface also widens the attack surface, letting malicious shell commands hide within natural-language skill files. We make three contributions. First, an adversarial skill-synthesis method using six LLMs across four families to transform 471 real-world shell commands into benign-appearing skills, released as a benchmark of 2,826 skills mapped to 11 MITRE ATT&CK tactics. Second, a reproducible evaluation pipeline coupling run stratification, evidence anchoring, a refusal veto, and a deterministic declared-intent override with a three-judge LLM-as-a-judge panel, validated against a blind human gold standard (Cohen's kappa = 0.85). Third, a large-scale characterization of two enterprise-grade agents across 5,629 completed runs. Gemini CLI is exploited in 95.5-96.1% of runs and Qwen Code in 71.6-74.0% (raw majority vote to declared-intent-corrected estimate, both within the human gold standard), nearly invariant to the generating model. Explicit safety recognition occurs in only 1.99% of runs. Enterprises must assess and mitigate skill-interface risk before adopting coding agents. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/awsm-research/AgentJailbreak

</details>


### [8] [Improving Debugging in Verification-Aware Languages Through Automated Fault Localization: A Case Study in Dafny](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05399)
*Álvaro Silva,Isabel Amaral,João Pascoal Faria,Alexandra Mendes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了验证感知语言（如Dafny）中的自动化故障定位，对比了基于状态和基于反例两种定位范式，并提出了一套从原始反例提取到多轨迹聚合的渐进式反例定位技术族。通过MutDafny生成变异数据集并以EXAM分数评估，发现反例驱动方法显著优于状态基方法，其中结构化单轨迹排序提升最大，多轨迹聚合进一步增强了鲁棒性和调试实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证感知语言（如Dafny）在验证失败时只能提供特定条件（如违反的后置条件）而非故障的根本原因。Dafny的反例功能虽能提供具体执行轨迹，但通常只暴露单条失败路径，开发者仍需手动翻阅整个轨迹来定位错误，缺乏自动化的故障定位手段。

Method: 论文从两个范式展开：1）状态基定位策略复制AutoFix的"快照"方法，通过推断不变量和谓词来识别可疑程序状态；2）反例基定位策略包含一族技术，从原始反例提取逐步升级到结构化单轨迹排序，再到多轨迹聚合。评估框架使用MutDafny从DafnyBench生成多种变异数据集，并用EXAM分数衡量定位效果。

Result: 反例基方法显著优于状态基定位。单轨迹上的结构化排序比原始反例输出带来最大的性能提升；多轨迹聚合通过增加覆盖率和减少求解器引入的路径偏差，进一步提升了鲁棒性和调试实用性。

Conclusion: 在验证感知语言中，有效的故障定位不仅取决于是否使用反例信息，还取决于这些信息如何被结构和多样化处理。反例信息的结构化组织和多维度汇聚是提升定位效果的关键因素。

Abstract: Verification-aware languages, like Dafny, integrate formal specifications directly into source code to enable static correctness checks. However, when verification fails, the feedback provided is often limited to the specific condition of the error, such as a violated postcondition, rather than the root cause of the fault. While Dafny's counterexample features provide concrete execution traces, these typically expose a single failing path per assertion failure, leaving the developer to manually look through the entire trace to locate the error.
  This paper investigates automated fault localization for verification-aware languages by comparing two paradigms: state-based and counterexample-based localization. Our state-based localization strategy replicates the ``snapshot'' methodology of AutoFix by inferring invariants and predicates to identify suspicious program states. The counterexample-based strategy consists of a family of techniques that progressively enrich the use of verifier output: from raw counterexample extraction, to structured single-trace ranking, and to multi-trace aggregation.
  To validate these methods, we present an evaluation framework using MutDafny to generate a diverse mutant dataset from DafnyBench and measure localization effectiveness using the EXAM score. Our results show that counterexample-based approaches substantially outperform state-based localization in this setting. Structured ranking over a single trace yields the largest improvement over raw counterexample output, while multi-trace aggregation provides additional gains in robustness and debugging utility by increasing coverage and reducing path bias introduced by the solver. These findings demonstrate that effective fault localization in verification-aware languages depends both on using counterexample information, and how that information is structured and diversified.

</details>


### [9] [Reasoning from Traces: Divergence-Guided Agentic Repair of WebAssembly Discrepancies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05521)
*Liyan Huang,Kaicheng Wang,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出WasmMend，第一个自动修复Native-Wasm功能差异的系统，通过差异轨迹分析定位分歧函数，再引导LLM代理进行根因推理和补丁生成，在真实C/C++项目上达到70.0%的修复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebAssembly承诺实现C/C++代码库的无缝跨平台复用，但实践中由于库实现差异或编译器bug，Wasm与原生二进制之间经常出现运行时差异。由于根因隐藏在平台级运行时之下，即使是先进的LLM修复代理也常常无法修复这些差异。

Method: WasmMend采用两阶段方法：首先通过新颖的差异轨迹分析方法定位Wasm与原生执行首次发生分歧的函数；然后基于该定位结果，引导LLM代理进行根因推理并生成消除分歧行为的补丁。

Result: 在真实C/C++项目上的实验表明，WasmMend达到70.0%的修复率，优于agentic基线（50.2%）和结合修复时LLM插桩的增强方法（54.5%）。

Conclusion: 差异引导的推理对跨平台修复具有重要价值，WasmMend验证了将无方向探索转化为聚焦推理任务的有效性，为Native-Wasm功能差异的自动化修复提供了可行方案。

Abstract: WebAssembly (Wasm) promises seamless reuse of C/C++ codebases as portable, fast, sandboxed binaries. In practice, however, this promise often falls short: recent studies show that cross-compiling the same C/C++ source to Wasm and native binaries frequently leads to runtime discrepancies, owing to library implementation differences or compiler bugs. Since the root causes lie in the platform-level runtime and are hidden beneath the source code, even state-of-the-art LLM-based repair agents often fail to fix these discrepancies. In this paper, we present WasmMend, the first system to automatically repair Native-Wasm functional discrepancies. WasmMend converts the undirected exploration to a focused reasoning task in two stages: First, a novel differential trace analysis approach localizes the function where Wasm and native executions initially diverge; guided by this localization, LLM agents then reason about the root causes and generate patches that eliminate the divergent behavior. Experiments on real-world C/C++ projects show that WasmMend achieves a fix rate of 70.0%, compared to 50.2% for the agentic baseline and 54.5\% for the approach augmented with repair-time LLM-based instrumentation, demonstrating the value of divergence-guided reasoning for cross-platform repair.

</details>


### [10] [Exploring Dependence, Overreliance, and Addiction Related Behaviors Associated with Large Language Model Use Among Software Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05561)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Italo Santos,Matheus de Moraes Leça,Cleyton Magalhaes,Mairieli Wessel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过对119名软件从业者的探索性调查，考察了他们在专业软件开发中使用大语言模型（LLM）时表现出的依赖、过度依赖和成瘾相关行为模式。研究发现参与者主要表现出功能性依赖，部分过度依赖迹象，而成瘾相关行为较少见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在软件开发中的广泛采用，其在提高生产力的同时也引发了关于开发者依赖、过度依赖和成瘾行为的担忧。然而，目前缺乏对该问题的系统实证研究，本研究旨在填补这一空白，深入理解软件工程师如何体验LLM的使用及其行为模式。

Method: 采用探索性调查方法，对119名软件从业者进行问卷调查。数据通过描述性统计和参与者开放式回答的定性主题分析进行处理和分析。

Result: 参与者主要描述功能性依赖，LLM因提供生产力和效率而被整合到日常软件工程活动中。响应中也出现与过度依赖一致的模式，特别是在优先使用LLM而非文档或同行咨询，但同时继续验证生成输出。成瘾相关行为的报告较少，主要反映为难以节制使用或对技术的情感依恋，而非控制力受损。

Conclusion: LLM正成为专业软件工程实践中的习惯性组成部分。虽然多数报告的使用是功能性的，但研究结果表明，通过支持信任校准、专业判断力和开发过程中的验证来促进适当依赖非常重要。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) has changed how software engineers perform everyday development activities. While these systems provide substantial support for tasks such as code generation, debugging, and documentation, their increasing integration into professional workflows has also raised questions regarding developers' reliance on these tools and the emergence of dependence, overreliance, and addiction-related behaviors. This study investigates how software engineers experience the use of LLMs during professional software development, with attention to behavioral patterns associated with dependence, overreliance, and addiction-related behaviors. An exploratory survey was conducted with 119 software practitioners. The data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and qualitative thematic analysis of participants' open-ended responses. Participants primarily described functional dependence, with LLMs becoming integrated into routine software engineering activities because of the productivity and efficiency they provide. Responses also suggested patterns consistent with overreliance, particularly through prioritizing LLMs over documentation or peer consultation while continuing to verify generated outputs. Reports associated with addiction-related behaviors were less common and primarily reflected difficulty moderating use or emotional attachment to the technology rather than impaired control. The findings suggest that LLMs are becoming a habitual component of professional software engineering practice. While most reported use appears functional, the results indicate the importance of promoting appropriate reliance by supporting trust calibration, professional judgment, and verification throughout software development.

</details>


### [11] [JTA: Joint Testability Architecture for Scenario-Based Validation of Safety-Critical Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05594)
*Wenyao Xue,Jiandi Wang,Yichen Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出联合可测试性架构（JTA），将场景、测试系统和被测系统三者作为统一分析对象，通过可控性、可观测性和可隔离性三个维度及三个域、三个桥和分析-设计-评估-优化循环来刻画验证能力，并通过ArduPilot失效安全验证案例说明其应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可测试性研究主要聚焦于被测工件本身（artifact-centric），缺乏对场景、测试系统与被测系统三者联合能力的架构级推理支持，导致安全关键软件验证充分性难以系统评估。

Method: 提出JTA（联合可测试性架构），将场景、测试系统和被测系统视为单一分析与设计对象；沿可控性、可观测性、可隔离性三个维度刻画验证能力；通过三个域、三个桥和分析-设计-评估-优化循环加以组织；引入场景契约、联合能力评估、验证盲区识别和面向桥的设计行动。

Result: 对ArduPilot失效安全验证的示例分析表明，链路丢失（link-loss）场景验证已相对成熟，而状态估计异常场景因证据对齐和归因语义较弱而更难验证。JTA可作为现有测试与安全分析技术的补充而非替代。

Conclusion: JTA为安全关键软件中基于场景的验证提供了架构层面的建模、设计与评估基础，弥补了现有方法在联合能力推理方面的不足。

Abstract: Validation adequacy in safety-critical software depends on more than the system under test. Critical scenarios must be constructed under controlled conditions, execution evidence must be aligned into verdict-ready form, and abnormal outcomes must be attributable to actionable causes. Existing testability research remains largely artifact-centric and offers little architectural support for reasoning about the combined capability of the scenario, the test system, and the system under test. Joint Testability Architecture (JTA) addresses this gap by treating those three elements as a single object of analysis and design. It characterizes validation capability along three dimensions--controllability, observability, and isolability--and organizes them through three domains, three bridges, and an analysis-design-evaluation-refinement loop. JTA also introduces scenario contracts, joint capability assessment, validation blind-spot identification, and bridge-oriented design actions that map capability gaps to concrete improvements in control points, evidence organization, and attribution boundaries. An illustrative analysis of ArduPilot failsafe validation shows that link-loss scenarios are comparatively mature, whereas state-estimation anomaly scenarios remain harder to validate because evidence alignment and attribution semantics are weaker. JTA is not a replacement for existing testing or safety-analysis techniques; it provides an architectural basis for modeling, designing, and assessing scenario-based validation in safety-critical software.

</details>


### [12] [Towards Competence-Based Management for Open Source Software Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05599)
*Sabahat Younas,Márcia Moraes,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于代码指标来评估开源软件贡献者能力水平的方法，并构建了一个预测任务所需能力等级的模型，旨在帮助识别和培养能够接替核心贡献者的候选人才。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件项目中核心贡献者的离职日益增多，威胁到项目的生存。寻找新贡献者接替工作既困难又耗时，主要原因之一是缺乏对潜在候选人能力水平的精确了解，以往的研究虽提供了一些能力指示，但对能力水平的理解不够深入，导致对贡献者能力的认知不足。

Method: 通过收集贡献中与源代码相关的代码指标来评估贡献者的能力，并提出了一个能够预测解决任务所需能力等级的能力模型。

Result: 通过合理评估贡献者的能力，可以识别并培训候选人以接替核心贡献者。作者提供了包含代码、数据和文档的完整复制包。

Conclusion: 基于代码指标的贡献者能力评估方法能够有效预测任务所需的能力等级，为识别和培养核心贡献者的替代人选提供了可行方案，有助于维护开源社区的可持续性。

Abstract: Contributors to Open Source Software (OSS) projects are vital to maintaining the health of both communities and projects. However, the number of projects experiencing core contributors' disengagement has increased to the point that it risks the projects' survival. Finding new contributors to replace the workforce is challenging and time-intensive due to several factors, including a lack of precise knowledge about potential candidates' competences, which may need to be confirmed through interviews and exams. Previous studies provided indications of the contributor's competences, although they lack depth in understanding competence levels, which can result in poor knowledge about the contributor's capabilities. To address this gap, we assess contributors' competence by collecting code metrics related to source code from contributions. We also propose a competence model able to predict the competence level required to solve tasks. By properly assessing contributors' competences, we can identify and train candidates to replace core contributors. Our replication package, including code, data, and documentation, is available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21605122

</details>


### [13] [Keeping Models and Code in Sync: Roundtrip Engineering for Tactical Domain-Driven Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05612)
*Weixing Zhang,Mario Herb,Wai Chung Dorothy Cheng,Michael Wagner,Bowen Jiang,Tianhai Liu,Anne Koziolek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出JDomInO工具链，通过共享元模型实现Java代码库与DDD领域模型的双向同步，正向生成和逆向重建两条路径保证模型与代码一致，并探讨其作为AI编程助手上下文层的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDD领域模型与代码在实际开发中容易发生漂移：代码变更超前于模型，或模型修订未能同步到代码库，导致共享词汇表失效，团队沟通和系统演进受阻。

Method: JDomInO采用双向同步工具链设计：正向路径从领域模型确定性生成Java代码结构；反向路径从现有Java代码重建领域模型。两条路径通过共享元模型（覆盖12种建筑块类型）实现连接。

Result: 正向路径已在酒店管理场景完成全面验证，覆盖元模型中全部12种建筑块类型；反向路径的映射逻辑已通过单元测试，端到端验证正在进行中。

Conclusion: JDomInO生成的结构化领域模型可作为AI代码助手的精度上下文层，帮助其理解聚合边界和DDD语义，弥补原始源代码无法传达的领域知识。

Abstract: Domain-Driven Design gives teams a shared vocabulary for complex business logic, but that vocabulary only stays useful as long as the model and the code agree with each other. In practice, they drift apart: code changes outpace the model, or model revisions never make it into the codebase. This paper presents JDomInO, a bidirectional synchronization toolchain for tactical DDD that keeps a Java codebase and its domain model connected through a shared metamodel, with the goal of keeping the two in sync as the system evolves. JDomInO generates Java code structure deterministically from a domain model (forward path) and reconstructs a domain model from existing Java code (reverse path). The forward path has been fully validated on a Hotel Management scenario covering all 12 building block types in the metamodel; the reverse path's mapping logic has passed unit testing, with end-to-end validation underway. We also outline how the structured domain model produced by JDomInO could serve as a precision context layer for AI code assistants, helping them respect aggregate boundaries and DDD semantics that raw source code alone does not convey.

</details>


### [14] [Agent-Based Test Assertion Generation via Diverse Perspective Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05822)
*Dong Wang,Qiaoyu Han,Lin Yang,Jianyi Zhou,Guangtai Liang,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了AssertMate，一个基于智能体的断言生成框架，通过实际值构造、多视角期望值预测和LLM裁判协作三个核心组件，显著提升了单元测试断言生成的准确性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的断言生成方法（如ChatAssert）存在准确率不高、过度依赖过采样、单次提示易受模型随机性影响等问题。

Method: AssertMate包含三个关键组件：(1)实际值构造——通过静态分析和类型感知启发式识别断言目标；(2)多视角期望值预测——结合代码生成、检索增强生成（RAG）和链式推理（CoT）三种智能体；(3)LLM-as-a-Judge协作机制——选择最合适的断言。

Result: 在Defects4J基准上，AssertMate在编译成功率和通过率上显著优于现有技术，并具有更高的缺陷检测能力。与EvoSuite集成后获得更优的变异覆盖率和kill数。消融实验证明了三个组件的显著且互补的贡献。

Conclusion: 聚合多种视角可以增强基于LLM的断言生成效果，验证了该方法在实际软件测试中的实用性和巨大潜力。

Abstract: Test assertions are critical elements of unit tests, serving as checkpoints to validate expected behavior and ensure software correctness. Numerous techniques have been proposed to automate assertion generation, with recent progress notably driven by large language models (LLMs). Despite the promise, existing approaches such as ChatAssert suffer from modest accuracy, heavy reliance on oversampling, and vulnerability to model randomness due to one-shot prompting. To address these limitations, we propose AssertMate, a novel agent-based assertion generation framework that enhances the quality and reliability of LLM-generated assertions through three key components: (1) actual value construction that identifies assertion targets via static analysis and type-aware heuristics; (2) multi-perspective expected value prediction using code generation, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning agents; and (3) an LLM-as-a-Judge collaboration mechanism to select the most appropriate assertion. Evaluation on the Defects4J benchmark demonstrates that AssertMate significantly outperforms state-of-the-art techniques in compilation success and pass rates, along with substantially higher bug detection capabilities. Integration with EvoSuite further validates AssertMate's practicality, yielding superior mutation coverage and kill counts. Ablation studies reveal that each of the three components makes a significant and complementary contribution to the overall performance. This work affirms the great potential of aggregating diverse perspectives to enhance the effectiveness of LLM-based assertion generation.

</details>


### [15] [CodeGrep: An RL-Trained Retrieval Agent for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05886)
*Wuya Chen,Yihao yang,Yang Cao,Yue Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeGrep，一个14B参数的文件检索智能体，通过GRPO端到端训练，在多轮并行grep/glob/read工具调用中高效定位候选文件，供下游编码智能体使用，在不降低解决率的情况下显著减少轮次和token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM编码智能体（如Claude Code、OpenHands）存在共同的低效问题：大量token预算浪费在查找待修补文件上而非实际修补。在SWE-Bench Verified上，一个30B的OpenHands智能体平均需要23轮和631K token才能解决一个问题，其中大量调用用于仓库探索（grep、glob、view_file）。需要专门的检索智能体来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 1) 提出CodeGrep，一个14B参数的检索智能体，使用GRPO进行端到端训练，能够发出多轮并行grep、glob和read工具调用并返回候选文件给冻结的下游编码智能体。2) 使用CATM从67K开源智能体轨迹中挖掘监督信号。3) 构建Git-worktree环境用于多轮智能体强化学习。4) 在advantage层而非reward层应用效率信号，以减少KL漂移。

Result: 在全部500个SWE-Bench Verified实例上，CodeGrep在保持解决率的同时显著提升效率：解决率27.0%对比无检索基线的25.8%，在已解决实例上减少15%轮次和19% token。检索器精度存在阈值效应：BM25精度0.375使智能体退化，Jina精度0.445为中性，CodeGrep精度0.677超过阈值开始降低卷动成本。

Conclusion: CodeGrep验证了专门的检索智能体能够有效解决编码智能体的文件查找瓶颈。精度是下游效用提升的关键因素，超过精度阈值后检索才能降低整体成本。在advantage层应用效率信号比reward层更优，减少KL漂移且更干净地转化为下游效率。作者将开源模型、训练管道、RL环境和评估工具。

Abstract: Modern LLM coding agents such as Claude Code and OpenHands share a common inefficiency: they spend much of their token budget finding the file to patch, rather than patching it. On SWE-Bench Verified, a 30B OpenHands agent averages 23 rounds and 631K tokens per resolved issue, with many calls spent on grep, glob, and view_file during repository exploration. We introduce CodeGrep, a 14B retrieval agent trained end-to-end with GRPO to issue multi-turn parallel grep, glob, and read tool calls and return candidate files to a frozen downstream coding agent.
  On all 500 SWE-Bench Verified instances, CodeGrep preserves resolve rate while substantially improving efficiency: 27.0% versus 25.8% for the no-retrieval baseline, with 15% fewer rounds and 19% fewer tokens on resolved instances. Across retrievers, downstream utility follows a precision threshold: BM25 with precision 0.375 degrades the agent, Jina with precision 0.445 is neutral, and CodeGrep with precision 0.677 crosses the threshold at which retrieval begins to reduce rollout cost. To enable this study, we mine supervision from 67K open-source agent trajectories using CATM and build a Git-worktree environment for multi-turn agent RL. In our setting, applying the efficiency signal at the advantage layer rather than the reward layer reduces KL drift and translates cleanly into downstream efficiency. We will release the model, training pipeline, RL environment, and evaluation harnesses.

</details>


### [16] [Mapping the Emerging Curriculum for AI-Assisted Software Engineering via Syllabus Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05898)
*Francis Geng,Anshul Shah,Mia Chen,Paul Denny,Juho Leinonen,Bill Griswold,Gerald Soosai Raj,Leo Porter*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过分析23门高校公开课程大纲，系统研究了生成式AI编程工具在软件工程教育中的教学现状，为未来课程设计提供实证依据和指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI编码工具正在重塑专业软件开发，大学开始设计相关课程帮助学生适应AI辅助开发工作流，但缺乏对这些课程的系统性实证研究，需要通过分析课程大纲来了解这一新兴课程领域的定义方式和发展方向。

Method: 收集并筛选了23份公开可获取的高年级学分制课程大纲和材料（要求明确涉及软件工程中的生成式AI），采用迭代式定性编码方法，对课程的学习目标、评估方式、主题内容和所用AI工具进行系统化特征分析。

Result: 分析揭示了这些课程之间的共性和差异，为研究者和教育者研究及开发未来课程提供了实证数据基础。

Conclusion: 通过对现有课程的系统分析，本文为生成式AI软件工程教育这一新兴课程领域提供了初步的实证画像，既展示了当前课程设计的共识，也暴露了存在的差异性，可为未来课程开发和教学研究提供参考方向。

Abstract: As Generative AI coding tools reshape professional software development, universities have begun designing courses to prepare students for AI-assisted development workflows. By analyzing the syllabi of these courses, we can gather empirical evidence about these courses, reveal how this emerging curricular area is being defined, and gain guidance for future curriculum design. We analyzed 23 publicly available syllabi and course materials of upper-division, credit-bearing courses that meet specific criteria, including explicitly addressing Generative AI in software engineering. Through iterative qualitative coding, we characterized courses' learning objectives, assessments, topics, and documented AI tools. Our analysis reveals commonalities and differences among these courses that allow researchers and educators to study and develop future courses.

</details>


### [17] [Escaping the Self-Repair Trap: Improving Test Oracle Generation via Dual-Context Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05917)
*Kefan Li,Hongyue Yu,Yuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DCAware框架，针对LLM在回归测试预言生成中迭代自修复导致"Self-Repair Trap"（模型趋向生成易满足但检测能力弱的断言）的问题，通过融合结构化静态上下文与选择性检索的动态状态，实现无需迭代、计算高效的预言生成，在保证执行成功率的同时提升故障发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的回归预言生成方法过度依赖迭代自修复和执行反馈，但优化执行成功只是代理目标，与生成强故障检测预言的真实目标不一致。这种错位会导致"Self-Repair Trap"——迭代修复使模型逐渐偏向生成容易满足但检测能力较弱的断言，从而损害预言质量。

Method: DCAware是一个非迭代框架，核心思想是优先提供高信噪比的上下文信息而非多轮修复。具体包括：1)整合结构化静态上下文；2)选择性检索动态状态信息；3)结合两者实现精确且鲁棒的预言生成，完全避免迭代反馈循环。

Result: 基于执行测试和变异测试的广泛实验表明，DCAware在故障揭示有效性上持续优于现有方法，同时保持高执行成功率，并以显著更低的计算成本超越先前方法。

Conclusion: 在回归预言生成场景中，提升上下文质量比增加迭代修复复杂度更为有效。DCAware验证了非迭代、上下文驱动的方法是一种更优的研究方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for regression-oracle completion, where a test prefix is given and the current program version is treated as expected behavior. Recent approaches increasingly rely on iterative self-repair and execution feedback, but optimizing execution success does not necessarily yield strong fault-revealing oracles. This objective, widely adopted in repair-based methods, serves only as a proxy and may be misaligned with the true goal of oracle generation. Such misalignment biases the repair process, giving rise to a feedback-driven degeneration that we term the Self-Repair Trap, where iterative repair progressively drives models toward assertions that are easier to satisfy but less effective at detecting faults. To address this issue, we propose DCAware, a computationally efficient, non-iterative framework that prioritizes high signal-to-noise contextual grounding over multi-round repair. DCAware integrates structured static context with selectively retrieved dynamic states, enabling precise and robust oracle generation without iterative feedback loops. Extensive experiments based on execution and mutation testing show that DCAware consistently improves fault-revealing effectiveness while maintaining high execution success, outperforming prior methods with substantially lower computational cost. Our results suggest that improving contextual quality is more effective than adding iterative repair complexity in the studied regression-oracle setting.

</details>


### [18] [Expertise-Based Developer Assignment for Long-Term Software Components in Open-Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05919)
*Faheem Ullah,Babar Shah,William Shanks,Ayesha Mohsin,Abrar Ullah*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Git提交历史建模开发者专业技能的Web应用，用于自动将开发者分配到新项目的长期组件任务中。测试表明，服务器端数据缓存可将最差情况速度提升9.86倍，72.4%的任务目标开发者排在前10名（共47人）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GitHub开源软件开发中，将任务分配给不匹配开发者专业技能的开发者会导致完成时间变慢和代码可维护性降低。虽然已有大量针对特定岗位的自动化任务分配研究，但针对新项目中按专业技能划分的长期组件任务分配研究较少。

Method: 构建一个Web应用，基于开发者以往的Git提交记录来建模其专业技能，并自动将其分配到新项目中的最优任务。系统支持多种后端模型，并采用服务器端数据缓存来优化性能。

Result: 系统性能因后端模型而异，服务器端数据缓存使最差情况速度提升了9.86倍。在47名候选开发者的测试中，72.4%的任务的目标开发者位列推荐前10名。

Conclusion: 基于Git提交历史的专业技能建模可用于自动分配开发者到新项目的长期组件任务，且系统在准确性和性能上均表现令人满意，缓存技术有效提升了最差场景下的响应速度。

Abstract: Open-source software development through GitHub has enabled countless software projects to be developed by developers from across the world. Assigning the right task to the right developer enables teams to work efficiently. Assigning a task which is not representative of a developer's expertise results in slower completion time and less maintainable code. Whilst much work has been done to automate this assignment for specific jobs, there is little work addressing the assignment of developers to expertise-specific long-term components of a new project. This paper produces a web application that models developers' expertise based on their previous Git commits and automatically assigns them to an optimal task within a new project. Testing showed that the system's speed varies depending on the back-end model used but server-side data caching improved worst-case speeds by a factor of 9.86. 72.4% of tasks had their target developer listed in the top 10, out of a possible 47.

</details>


### [19] [Sensor-Level Fault Diagnosis for Automotive Software Validation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05921)
*Mohammad Abboush,Hamza Ouarrad,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于开源指令微调大语言模型（LLM）的两阶段框架，用于汽车软件HIL平台验证中的故障检测与诊断。首先通过自动需求检查筛选违规记录，然后仅对这些记录使用4-bit低秩适配的LLM进行故障定位并生成书面解释。在汽油机案例研究中，最小模型（2B参数）与最大模型（8B参数）均达到81.6%准确率，整个适配与评估周期可在单块商用加速器上完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车软件在HIL平台上的预系列验证产生大量多变量传感器记录，人工审查无法应对规模化验证的需求。现有阈值工具只能报告偏差发生但不能识别其性质或定位来源；数据驱动分类器虽然准确但依赖大规模标注数据且决策不透明，难以满足ISO 26262的可追溯性要求。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段在dSPACE实时平台上进行自动化需求检查，隔离违反安全需求的记录；第二阶段仅对违规记录进行检查，将滑动窗口信号转换为统计、关系和上下文描述符，嵌入固定提示模板，由4-bit低秩适配（LoRA）的LLM映射到故障位置并附带书面推理说明。测试了四个参数规模从20亿到80亿的模型家族，在涵盖六种注入故障类别的汽油发动机案例上进行评估。

Result: 最小模型（2B参数）与最大模型（8B参数）在诊断准确率上持平，均为81.6%；而一个规模相当的模型未能收敛。这表明在特定任务适配下，诊断能力取决于收敛性而非参数数量。整个适配与评估周期可在单块商用加速器上完成。

Conclusion: 开源指令微调LLM在任务特定适配下可以作为数据高效且可解释的故障检测与诊断引擎，在验证循环中发挥作用。诊断能力的关键是模型收敛性而非参数规模，较小的模型在合理适配下也能达到与大规模模型相当的性能，同时满足ISO 26262对可追溯性的要求。

Abstract: The pre-series validation of automotive software on hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) platforms produces large volumes of multivariate sensor recordings whose assessment against functional safety requirements exceeds what manual review can sustain at campaign scale. Threshold-based tooling reports that a deviation has occurred but neither identifies its nature nor locates its source, while data-driven classifiers, although accurate, rely on large labelled datasets and return opaque decisions that sit uneasily with the traceability demanded by ISO 26262. This study examines whether open-source instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs), given a textual description of sensor behaviour, can serve as data-efficient and interpretable engines for fault detection and diagnosis inside the validation loop. A two-phase framework is proposed: automated requirement checking on a dSPACE real-time platform first isolates the recordings that violate a safety requirement, and only these are inspected, with sliding windows of the signals reduced to statistical, relational, and contextual descriptors, embedded in a fixed prompt, and mapped by a 4-bit low-rank-adapted LLM to a fault location accompanied by a written justification. Four model families ranging from two to eight billion parameters were adapted and tested on a gasoline-engine case study spanning six injected fault classes. The smallest model matched the largest at 81.6\% accuracy, whereas a comparably sized model failed to converge, indicating that diagnostic competence under task-specific adaptation follows convergence rather than parameter count, with the entire adapt-and-evaluate cycle fitting on a single commodity accelerator.

</details>


### [20] [AgentExecutor: Partial Code Execution via Agentic Context Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05959)
*Junkai Chen,Chengran Yang,Xing Hu,Zhenhao Li,Xin Xia,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentExecutor，一个基于多智能体框架的部分代码执行方法。它通过三阶段设计（执行环境准备、动态探索迭代精化、前缀演化程序合成），利用LLM智能体的自主思考、行动和反馈能力，在更丰富的动作空间中探索，并采用自适应优化策略（覆盖率引导的上下文剪枝和前缀演化），在两个数据集上实现了高达94%和90%的代码覆盖率，相比Treefix提升了19.9%和13.8%，同时显著降低了执行时间和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分代码执行是动态程序分析的关键任务，但现有方法（如LExecutor和Treefix）存在两个主要局限：(1)动作空间和反馈机制受限；(2)优化策略僵化，无法灵活适应不同的代码片段，这限制了部分代码执行的效率和有效性。

Method: 提出AgentExecutor多智能体框架，包含三个核心阶段：(1)执行环境准备——为代码执行构建基础环境；(2)动态探索与迭代精化——利用LLM智能体自主思考、行动和获取反馈的能力，在更丰富的动作空间中探索，如创建资源文件和解决环境配置问题；(3)前缀演化——通过程序合成技术进行前缀的迭代优化。同时采用覆盖率引导的上下文剪枝和自适应优化策略来系统提升部分代码的执行质量。

Result: 在两个广泛使用的数据集（Stack Overflow代码片段和开源项目代码）上，AgentExecutor分别实现了94%和90%的代码覆盖率，优于最先进方法Treefix达19.9%和13.8%；执行时间减少了最多80.3%，成本降低了最多56.6%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明AgentExecutor为部分代码执行提供了有效且高效的解决方案，通过多智能体协同和自适应优化策略，在覆盖率、执行时间和成本等多个维度上都显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Executing code snippets is essential for dynamic program analysis, but it remains challenging to execute an arbitrary code snippet due to issues like missing context and incomplete dependencies. Existing approaches to partial code execution, such as LExecutor and Treefix, leverage the power of language models to infer missing information and enable execution. However, they suffer from (i) limited action spaces and feedback, and (ii) rigid optimization strategies, which restrict their effectiveness and efficiency. In this paper, we propose AgentExecutor, a novel multi-agent framework for partial code execution. Our approach introduces a three-phase design: execution environment preparation, dynamic exploration with iterative refinement, and prefix evolution via program synthesis. Supported by the power of LLM agents who can think, act, and get feedback iteratively, AgentExecutor is able to autonomously explore a richer action space, enabling diverse operations such as creating resource files and resolving environment configuration. Furthermore, it adopts adaptive optimization strategies, including coverage-guided context pruning and prefix evolution via program synthesis, to systematically improve the execution quality of partial code. We evaluate AgentExecutor on two widely used datasets comprising Stack Overflow snippets and open-source project code. The results show that AgentExecutor achieves up to 94% and 90% code coverage, outperforming the state-of-the-art approach Treefix by 19.9% and 13.8%, respectively. In addition, AgentExecutor significantly reduces execution time (by up to 80.3%) and cost (by up to 56.6%). These findings demonstrate that AgentExecutor provides an effective and efficient solution for partial code execution.

</details>


### [21] [LangChoiceBench: Measuring and Explaining Programming-Language Choice in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06041)
*Lukas Twist,Twm Stone,Helen Yannakoudakis,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了LangChoiceBench，一个用于衡量大语言模型在项目级代码生成中Python偏好程度的基准测试，覆盖28个项目、7个软件领域，评估了25个LLM，发现模型普遍过度选择Python、推荐与实现一致性低，并识别出"幻影证据"和推理与实现矛盾等失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在项目级代码生成中表现出强烈的Python偏好，但目前缺乏系统化的方法来衡量这一行为在新模型中的表现，因此需要构建一个标准化基准来量化这一现象。

Method: 构建LangChoiceBench基准，涵盖28个项目中Python往往不是最优选择的7个软件领域；评估25个多样化的LLM；分析9,826条推理轨迹，考察Python偏好、推荐-实现一致性和语言多样性三个维度。

Result: 发现Python仍被严重过度选择；推荐-实现一致性较低；较小的开源模型通常表现更强的Python偏好和较低的语言多样性；大多数Python选择是自动的或主要出于便利性驱动，而非显式考虑项目需求；部分案例中模型编造支持选择Python的上下文证据（幻影证据），或生成的代码与推理中选择的语言相矛盾。

Conclusion: LLM在项目级代码生成中存在系统性Python偏见，需要通过基准测试来度量和改进这一行为，特别是识别出的幻影证据和推理-实现不一致是值得关注的失败模式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been shown to exhibit strong Python preferences when generating project-level code, but there is currently no systematic way to measure this behaviour across new models. To bridge this gap, we introduce LangChoiceBench, a project-level code-generation benchmark for measuring Python preference, recommendation-implementation consistency, and language diversity. LangChoiceBench covers 28 projects across seven software areas where Python is often a poor default. We evaluate 25 diverse LLMs and find that Python remains heavily over-selected, recommendation-implementation consistency is low, and smaller open-weight models generally show stronger Python preference and lower language diversity. We further analyse 9,826 reasoning traces and find that most Python choices are automatic or driven primarily by ease, rather than explicit consideration of project requirements. In a smaller but important set of cases, models fabricate contextual support for choosing Python - a failure mode we call phantom evidence - or produce code that contradicts the language selected in their own reasoning.

</details>


### [22] [In Terms of Explainability: Refining Requirements for Self-Explainable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06049)
*Arno Leue,Akhila Bairy,Maike Schwammberger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对自主软件系统缺乏统一可解释性定义和开发指南的问题，提出了一套统一的可解释性定义和结构化的可解释性需求，并通过分析所得分类法，将解释质量（即解释的正确性）纳入统一定义中，以支持正式标准的制定和系统的认证与审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主软件系统日益复杂且承担更多责任，但缺乏透明性和可解释性；目前没有普遍适用的可解释性定义和开发指南，且欧盟AI法案和IEEE标准已认识到需要制定可解释性标准。

Method: 通过分析和组合现有定义提出统一的可解释性定义；提出构建（自）可解释系统所需的结构化可解释性需求；分析所得分类法并将解释质量（解释的正确性）纳入统一定义中。

Result: 提出了统一的可解释性定义和结构化可解释性需求，建立了包含解释正确性维度的分类法，支持（自）可解释系统正式标准的开发。

Conclusion: 该统一分类法的建立有助于推动可解释系统的正式标准制定，也是迈向可解释系统认证与审计的有益一步。

Abstract: Autonomous and software-intensive systems have been growing in occurrence, complexity, and assumed responsibility. Due to the high complexity of these systems, properties like transparency and explainability must be a focus of investigation. To date, no universally applicable definition and guide for the development of (self-)explainable systems exists. A need for explainability standards has already been recognized in the EU AI Act and the IEEE Transparency Standard 7001-2021. To address this need, we propose unified definitions in explainability based on an analysis and combination of existing definitions. Additionally, we present structured explainability requirements that are necessary to build (self-)explainable systems. By analysing the resulting taxonomy, we propose the incorporation of explanation goodness and thus correctness of explanations into the unified definitions. With our approach, we support the development of formal standards for (self-)explainable systems. Establishing such a uniform taxonomy also is a beneficial step towards certifying or auditing explainable systems.

</details>


### [23] [DCAS: Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding to Internalize Planning across Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06113)
*Kishanthan Thangarajah,Boyuan Chen,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示了CLI软件工程智能体在单一训练环境（OpenHands）下微调导致跨脚手架性能退化的问题，提出DCAS（解耦CLI智能体脚手架）拦截层方案，通过规划感知轨迹采集和跨脚手架评估来弥合差距，实证表明规划是高性能关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源生态中的CLI软件工程智能体训练轨迹几乎全部在OpenHands环境下收集，导致模型在非训练脚手架上部署时性能显著退化，而未经微调的基础模型不存在此差异，说明差距是微调导致的且与训练脚手架约定相关。

Method: 提出DCAS（Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding），一种后端替换拦截层，在不修改脚手架的前提下路由任意CLI脚手架与任意后端模型之间的API流量，支持跨脚手架评估和规划感知轨迹收集。区分显式规划（执行前产生的一等公民计划）和隐式规划（塑造整个智能体循环执行的结构约定），并通过规划源干预实验进行控制验证。

Result: 规划源干预实验确认规划质量是高杠杆组件，其增益超过观测到的跨脚手架性能下降。基于单脚手架下少量DCAS收集的规划感知轨迹微调的模型，在非训练脚手架上一致获得性能提升，且两种规划感知在训练数据中可经验性分离。

Conclusion: 要弥合跨脚手架性能差距，需将规划从固定的脚手架工件转变为可学习的模型能力，而不是依赖特定脚手架的结构性约定；DCAS为跨脚手架评估和规划感知训练数据收集提供了有效基础设施。

Abstract: CLI-based software-engineering agents have matured rapidly, yet the open ecosystem has converged on a single training environment: trajectory datasets used to fine-tune open models are collected almost exclusively under OpenHands. Models fine-tuned on this data score well under OpenHands but degrade substantially when deployed under any non-training scaffold. Untrained base models do not show this divergence, indicating the gap is fine-tuning-induced and tied to the conventions of the training scaffold. We argue that a load-bearing scaffold-specific behavior is planning structure, in two senses this paper distinguishes: explicit planning, a pre-execution plan produced as a first-class artifact, and implicit planning, the structural conventions that shape execution throughout the agent loop. Under this hypothesis, closing the gap requires moving planning from a fixed scaffold artifact to a learned model capability. We introduce Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding (DCAS), a backend-substitution interception layer that routes API traffic between any CLI scaffold and any backend model without modifying the scaffold, enabling cross-scaffold evaluation and planning-aware trajectory collection. Using DCAS, a controlled plan-source intervention confirms planning quality is a high-leverage component, with gains exceeding the cross-scaffold drops we observe. A model fine-tuned on a small set of DCAS-collected planning-aware trajectories under a single scaffold gains consistently across non-training scaffolds, and the two senses of planning are empirically separable in training data.

</details>


### [24] [Learning Globally Reusable Skills for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06153)
*Chen Yang,Jiashuo Tian,Ziqi Wang,Xinyin Liu,Meiru Ye,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出GSE（Globalized Skill Evolution）框架，通过技能关系图和基于聚类的技能整合来解决LLM代理技能演化中的过拟合和泛化问题，在两个软件工程任务及工业部署中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理技能演化方法将技能演化视为局部更新序列，忽略了技能间关系，且容易产生过拟合的技能更新，无法跨任务泛化。

Method: GSE框架包含两个核心组件：1）维护技能关系图（SRG）以显式建模并共同演化技能间关系，保持技能库的一致性；2）基于聚类的技能整合，从局部更新中抽象可复用能力，并通过重放驱动的验证防止过拟合和行为回退。

Result: 在bug暴露测试生成和误报错误报告过滤两个软件工程任务上，GSE在OpenHands和mini-SWE-agent两个编码代理上均取得最佳精确率、召回率和F1分数。测试生成的精确率和召回率分别提升6.1%~34.1%和31.8%~180.0%，误报过滤分别提升15.4%~96.4%和13.1%~19.8%。工业部署中F1分数提升61.4%。

Conclusion: GSE通过全局化的技能演化方法，有效解决了传统局部更新策略的局限，在多个代理和任务上展现出优异的性能和泛化能力，验证了其在实际工业场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Automated skill evolution enables Large Language Model (LLM) agents to continuously improve without expensive retraining. However, existing approaches typically treat skill evolution as a sequence of local updates, overlooking relationships among skills and often producing overfitted skill updates that fail to generalize across tasks. We propose GSE, a globalized skill evolution framework that jointly optimizes skill compatibility and skill generalization. To preserve consistency across the skill bank, GSE maintains a Skill Relation Graph (SRG) that explicitly models and co-evolves inter-skill relationships. To improve generalization, GSE performs cluster-based skill consolidation to abstract reusable capabilities from local updates and employs replay-driven verification to prevent overfitting and behavioral regressions. We evaluate GSE on two representative software engineering tasks: bug-revealing test generation and false-positive bug report filtering. Across two state-of-the-art coding agents, OpenHands and mini-SWE-agent, GSE consistently achieves the best precision, recall, and F1-score. Compared with existing evolution techniques, GSE improves precision and recall by 6.1%~34.1% and 31.8%~180.0% for test generation, and by 15.4%~96.4% and 13.1%~19.8% for false-positive filtering. Deployment on an internal industrial agent further yields a 61.4% improvement in F1-score, demonstrating the effectiveness and generalizability of GSE for evolving effective skills.

</details>


### [25] [Predicting Agile Success: The Critical Few Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06228)
*Ridewaan Hanslo,Maureen Tanner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究采用实证主义方法，通过PLS-SEM分析识别敏捷软件开发项目的关键成功因素（CSF），开发了一个可用于评估和衡量项目成功的新模型。研究发现少数关键因素对敏捷项目成功有显著贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然敏捷项目比传统软件开发项目管理方法更成功，但整体成功率仍然较低，大量项目仍被认为面临挑战或失败。这一低成功率归因于项目未严格依据敏捷项目关键成功因素（CSF）和当代项目成功标准进行评估。

Method: 采用实证主义（positivist）研究方法，从敏捷从业者的视角调查敏捷软件开发项目的关键成功因素。使用PLS-SEM（SmartPLS）分析数据并检验假设，以识别各构念与项目成功标准之间的显著关系。

Result: 研究发现少数关键因素对敏捷项目成功有显著贡献，并开发了一个可用于评估和衡量项目成功的新模型。

Conclusion: 该研究开发的新模型可用于评估和衡量敏捷软件开发项目的成功，为实践者提供了一种评估项目关键成功因素的工具。

Abstract: While Agile projects are more successful than traditional software development project management approaches, their overall success rate remains relatively low, with a high percentage of projects still deemed challenged or failed. This low project success rate is attributed to projects not being rigorously evaluated against critical success factors (CSF) of Agile projects and contemporary project success criteria. To identify the CSF that contribute to Agile software development project success as perceived by Agile practitioners, this study used a positivist approach to investigate the CSF of Agile software development projects. PLS-SEM with SmartPLS was used to analyse the data and test the hypotheses to identify significant relationships between the constructs and project success criteria. This research found that a few critical factors significantly contribute to Agile project success. The study developed a novel model that can be used to evaluate and measure project success.

</details>


### [26] [Automatic Translation of Unstructured Requirements into Linear Temporal Logic through Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06287)
*Alexandra Newcomb,Omar Ochoa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了六种现代大语言模型（LLMs）在将非结构化自然语言需求自动转换为线性时序逻辑（LTL）公式任务中的表现，通过少样本提示策略在15个异构需求上生成450个候选公式，结果显示通用LLMs无需任务特定微调即可在此任务上取得实用性能水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非结构化自然语言需求自动转换为形式化规范仍是需求工程和形式化方法领域的挑战，特别是对于安全关键和任务关键系统，其验证依赖于数学上精确的规范。研究希望探索现成的通用大语言模型能否帮助弥合这一差距。

Method: 采用少样本提示策略，对六种现代LLMs在包含15个结构各异需求的异构基准上进行评估。每个需求-模型对独立生成5次，共收集450个候选LTL公式。通过人工语义评估、pass@k（k=1,3,5）以及衡量随机试验间语法可复现性的自一致性指标来评估性能。同时配合模型生成的自然语言解释和基于时间线的LTL可视化来评估非专家可理解性。

Result: 研究结果表明，当前通用LLMs在非结构化自然语言到LTL的转换任务上可以达到具有实际意义（practically significant）的性能水平，无需任务特定的微调。

Conclusion: 现代LLMs正在成为半自动形式化工作流中可行的前端助手，可用于辅助需求到形式化规范的转换过程。

Abstract: Automatically translating unstructured natural language requirements into formal specifications remains a challenge in requirements engineering and formal methods, particularly for safety- and mission-critical systems whose verification depends on mathematically precise specifications. This paper evaluates whether contemporary off-the-shelf Large Language Models (LLMs) can help bridge this gap by generating Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) formulas directly from unstructured requirements. The study examines six modern LLMs using a few-shot prompting strategy on a heterogeneous benchmark of 15 structurally varied requirements. Five independent generations were collected for each requirement-model pair, yielding 450 candidate LTL formulas in total. Performance was assessed through manual semantic evaluation, pass@k for k in {1, 3, 5}, and a self-consistency measure capturing syntactic reproducibility across stochastic trials. The results indicate that current general-purpose LLMs can achieve practically significant performance on the unstructured NL-to-LTL task without task-specific fine-tuning. The study also considers understandability for non-experts by pairing generated formulas with model-produced natural language explanations and discussing the complementary use of timeline-based LTL visualization. The findings suggest that modern LLMs are becoming viable front-end assistants for semi-automated formalization workflows.

</details>

### [27] [MS-MLB: An Open Machine Learning Benchmark for Blood-Based MS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05196)
*Adam Simson,Ankush Dutta,Quang Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MS-MLB，一个基于GSE17048全血RNA表达数据的多发性硬化症（MS）机器学习分类基准，采用防泄漏的标准化评估流程，Gradient Boosting在留出集上取得MS Research Score 93.83、AUC-ROC 0.989的最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MS的诊断依赖临床评估、MRI和实验室证据，血RNA数据虽含有疾病相关免疫信号但不能替代临床诊断。已有研究将机器学习应用于MS血液转录组数据，但缺乏一个可复现、防泄漏的开放基准用于MS与健康对照的分类研究。

Method: 使用公开GSE17048队列构建MS与健康对照分类任务，在共享且防泄漏的流水线下评估多种机器学习算法，包括嵌套交叉验证、独立的留出集、自助法置信区间、ROC和PR分析、校准度量，并引入探索性的MS Research Score，及内置外部模型提交路径。

Result: Gradient Boosting在留出集上排名第一：MS Research Score为93.83，AUC-ROC为0.989，灵敏度0.950，特异性0.778，F1分数0.927，Brier分数0.050。

Conclusion: MS-MLB是首个基于GSE17048全血RNA表达数据、聚焦MS与健康对照分类且内置外部模型提交流程的开放基准，但该分数仅用于研究比较，未经临床验证。

Abstract: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is diagnosed through clinical assessment, magnetic resonance imaging, laboratory evidence when appropriate, and exclusion of better explanations. Blood RNA expression data may contain disease associated immune signal, but a blood RNA classifier cannot be treated as a replacement for clinical diagnosis. This paper presents MS-MLB (Multiple Sclerosis Machine Learning Benchmark), a reproducible open benchmark for machine learning based MS research classification from whole blood RNA expression data. MS-MLB uses the public GSE17048 cohort, converts it into an MS versus healthy control task, and evaluates multiple algorithms under a shared, leakage controlled pipeline that a researcher can rerun without reconfiguring the evaluation. The evaluation includes nested cross-validation, an untouched stratified holdout set, bootstrap confidence intervals, ROC and precision recall analysis, calibration measurement, and an exploratory MS Research Score. In the final benchmark summary, Gradient Boosting ranked first by MS Research Score on the holdout set, with an MS Research Score of 93.83, AUC-ROC of 0.989, sensitivity of 0.950, specificity of 0.778, $F_{1}$ score of 0.927, and Brier score of 0.050. Prior studies have applied machine learning to MS blood transcriptomic data, including PBMC stage classification and whole blood diagnostic signature modeling. The contribution here is different and narrower. To our knowledge, MS-MLB is the first open benchmark focused on MS versus healthy control classification from GSE17048 whole blood RNA expression data with a documented external model submission pathway built into the framework. The score is intended for research comparison only and has not been clinically validated. The benchmark is accessible here: https://github.com/duckyquang/MS-MLB.

</details>


### [28] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFTER框架，通过从冻结预测模型的残差中挖掘可解释的纠错特征，驱动轻量级后置校正器，在不微调主干网络的情况下显著提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的预训练预测模型常常以结构化、可重复的方式失败，而通过微调修复这些失败代价高昂。因此需要一种轻量级的后置纠错方法。

Method: CRAFTER提出纠错特征发现（corrective feature discovery）概念，与以往对数据生成过程建模的方法不同，它建模模型失败过程。框架包含两个互补的特征生成器：基于原始输入通道的组合搜索，以及基于大语言模型（LLM）提出的命名特征组合、二元标志和简短可执行代码。所有候选特征通过统一的验证集门控接受或拒绝，最终由验证集选择的校正器应用接受的特征或保持预测不变。

Result: 在6个公开数据集和6个冻结主干网络上，CRAFTER在每个特征预算下都超越了所有专门的特征工程系统，比单独使用校正器时改进效果大约翻倍，在最弱骨干网上将误差降低高达27%。这些增益在不同LLM后端下稳健，且在微调后的骨干网络上依然有效。

Conclusion: CRAFTER是一种有效的纠错特征挖掘框架，不仅能显著提升冻结预测模型的性能，还因其源无关的流水线设计，可作为评估和归因不同特征工程系统贡献的通用工具。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [29] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种概率编程语言，用于构建基于大语言模型（LLM）的应用程序流程，使开发者能够量化和传播整个应用流程中的不确定性，并无需修改业务逻辑即可尝试不同的推理扩展技术。论文通过实验研究和Rocq定理证明智能体的案例研究展示了该能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的应用在输出准确性和置信度方面存在不足，缺乏明确的不确定性度量。这种不确定性在多次LLM调用和工具调用的流程中会被放大，导致开发者和最终用户难以信任结果。

Method: 提出一种概率语言用于编程LLM流程，使开发者能够量化和传播不准确性，并在不增加额外代码的情况下尝试不同的推理扩展（inference scaling）技术。

Result: 通过实验研究证明了该能力的有效性，并展示了构建Rocq定理证明智能体的案例研究。

Conclusion: 概率语言能够帮助开发者更好地量化和传播LLM流程中的不确定性，提升应用的可信度，并灵活探索推理扩展策略。

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [30] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CGTime，一个4B参数的计算接地时间序列-语言模型。它通过解耦感知与描述来解决自监督陷阱：用确定性代码从真实多变量时间序列中计算统计量，再由LLM将这些预计算事实描述为语言。在多元时间序列理解任务上超越更大规模的通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法面临三难困境：可靠、真实、可扩展三者不可兼得。原因有二：(1) 自监督陷阱——让LLM读序列写描述，数据质量受限于模型自身感知能力，数据永远无法教会模型超出标注者已知的知识；(2) 大多数数据集仅使用单一变量，而关键的跨通道相关性、领先-滞后结构、共现异常等模式只在多变量场景中显现，恰好暴露了LLM标注能力的极限。

Method: 将感知与描述解耦：使用确定性代码从真实开源多变量时间序列中计算一组统计量，然后由LLM将这些预计算的事实转换成语义描述。感知（LLM不擅长的部分）交给计算处理，LLM专注于表达。

Result: CGTime在多元理解任务上超越更大规模的通用模型：在保留基准上取得最佳多元事实得分（0.283 vs GPT-4o-mini的0.173和GPT-5.4-nano的0.203），差异通过Holm校正的配对显著性检验；生成的描述中可验证数值事实更准确，覆盖的统计属性范围更广。

Conclusion: 通过计算与语言的解耦设计，可以在小规模模型上同时实现可靠性、真实性和可扩展性，突破自监督陷阱对多模态时间序列-语言对齐的根本限制。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [31] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiSR框架，将3D感知与推理显式解耦，利用现成感知模型构建结构化3D证据，并通过LoRA微调LLM在该证据上进行推理，在空间推理基准上取得有竞争力性能，同时提升了可解释性、模块化和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过大规模训练联合获取隐式3D感知和推理能力，但这种方法成本高昂且可解释性差。作者观察到感知模型擅长连续3D几何估计，而LLM擅长组合和符号推理，二者能力互补，因此尝试显式分离感知与推理。

Method: 提出DiSR框架，包含两个关键步骤：1）使用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化的3D证据；2）通过LoRA微调LLM，使其仅基于显式几何证据进行推理。无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂的工具使用策略。

Result: 在流行的空间推理基准上，DiSR取得了具有竞争力的性能，与端到端方法相当，同时无需大规模3D VQA训练。此外，DiSR在可解释性、模块化和计算效率方面均有显著优势。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知与推理是空间智能的一种可扩展且有效的替代范式，相比端到端建模更具优势，为空间推理提供了一个更高效、可解释的新方向。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>

*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出因子化生成模型中将潜在风格变量边缘分布匹配到高斯先验并不能保证其与类别信息无关，并系统证明了这一常见解释的错误性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因子化生成模型普遍通过将风格潜变量z_s的边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验来声称风格表示与类别信息独立，但这种解释在理论上不成立，需要严格审视。

Method: 推导出边缘分布匹配仅是因子化采样的四个必要条件之一；通过案例模型和四个代表性潜变量基线进行实证，采用线性探针、聚类准确率和外部类别条件生成评估泄漏程度；测试六种扰动（容量、课程、先验几何、监督等）及四种缓解策略。

Conclusion: 仅基于风格潜变量边缘分布的散度无法证明其与类别标签的独立性，报告边缘统计量不足以验证因子化生成模型中声称的属性。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>
