We consider the usage loss terms due to failures, to checkpointing, and to restart costs, and update the framework of Daly (2006) allowing users to specify optimal checkpointing usage intervals that minimize such losses. We derive the machine computational efficiency, which specifies the expected fractional resource allocation that is available for scientific computation. We illustrate the methodology using one year of production runtime data from the \emph{Frontier} supercomputer at Oak Ridge National Laboratory.

</details>


### [103] [Serverless platform driven CPU loadbalancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05633)
*Abdul Rehman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个由Serverless平台驱动的CPU负载均衡框架，通过基于SchedExt(SCX)构建的自定义Linux调度器，使控制平面能够直接影响CPU调度决策。实验表明八域配置可实现约15%的能耗降低，同时调用成本仅增加5%，在重负载下请求延迟比默认CFS调度器降低高达50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serverless平台拥有函数调用和资源利用的全局视图，但现有系统的CPU调度决策仍主要受限于操作系统调度器，导致平台层面的全局信息无法被充分利用来优化调度效果。

Method: 基于SchedExt(SCX)构建自定义Linux调度器，引入可配置的调度域和共享接口。控制平面根据函数历史到达间隔将函数分配到不同调度域；每个域内采用单队列负载均衡策略结合虚拟时间优先化策略，以改善任务放置并减少忙轮询任务的干扰。

Result: 八域配置达到最佳权衡：系统能耗降低约15%，调用成本仅增加5%；在重负载工作负载下，请求延迟相比默认Linux CFS调度器降低高达50%。

Conclusion: 将CPU调度决策暴露给Serverless控制平面，可以在保持调度灵活性的同时提升能源效率和工作负载性能，证明了平台驱动调度的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Serverless platforms maintain a global view of function invocations and resource utilization, yet existing systems largely restrict CPU scheduling decisions to the operating system scheduler. This paper presents a serverless platform-driven CPU load balancing framework that enables the control plane to directly influence CPU scheduling through a custom Linux scheduler built on SchedExt(SCX). The proposed scheduler introduces configurable scheduling domains and a shared interface that allows the control plane to assign functions to domains based on their historical inter-arrival times. Within each domain, a single-queue load-balancing strategy combined with a virtual-time prioritization policy improves task placement while reducing interference from busy-polling tasks. Results show that an eight-domain configuration achieves the best trade-off, reducing system energy consumption by approximately 15% while increasing invocation cost by only 5%. Under heavily loaded workloads, the proposed scheduler also reduces request latency by up to 50% compared to the default Linux Completely Fair Scheduler (CFS). These results demonstrate that exposing CPU scheduling decisions to the serverless control plane can improve both energy efficiency and workload performance while preserving scheduling flexibility.

</details>


### [104] [RepoOMP: Repository-Aware Hotspot OpenMP Parallelization via Dependency-Aware Context Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05855)
*Yongjie Qian,Ke Gao,Zhibin Zhang,Shaohui Peng,Ling Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RepoOMP是一个混合框架，通过构建多粒度属性性能图（MAP）和结构化变换上下文（STC），在规则验证和LLM代理之间路由热点的并行化任务，在951个热点上实现了8.23×到8.96×的平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成熟代码仓库中热点的OpenMP并行化仍然很困难，因为循环安全性和优化收益往往依赖于非局部证据。基于规则的工具在合法性无法局部证明时会欠并行化，而基于代理的方法在检索遗漏关键依赖或包含无关代码时变得不稳定。

Method: RepoOMP构建多粒度属性性能图（MAP），在确定性规则和LLM代理之间路由热点，并构造结构化变换上下文（STC），向模型暴露依赖事实而不淹没模型于无关仓库文本。

Result: 在951个来自NPB、BOTS、FFmpeg、NCNN和GROMACS的热点中，372个热点被接受（含330个真实仓库热点）。NPB平均加速8.23×，BOTS平均加速8.96×，跨骨干平均5.25×，相对Claude Code基线提升18-28%加速比，降低47-68%的代理端token成本。330个被接受的真实仓库热点中位加速为2.25×。

Conclusion: RepoOMP提供了一种证据引导的工作流，用于仓库环境中的热点并行化，在并行化成功率和效率上均优于基线方法。

Abstract: OpenMP parallelization of hotspots in mature repositories remains difficult because loop safety and optimization payoff often depend on non-local evidence. Rule-based tools under-parallelize when legality is not locally provable, while agent-based approaches become unstable when retrieval misses decisive dependencies or includes irrelevant code. We present RepoOMP, a hybrid framework that recovers parallelization-relevant evidence before generation. RepoOMP builds a Multi-granularity Attributes Performance graph (MAP), routes hotspots between deterministic rules and an LLM agent, and constructs a Structured Transformation Context (STC) that exposes dependency facts without flooding the model with unrelated repository text. We evaluate RepoOMP on 951 profiled hotspots from NPB, BOTS, FFmpeg, NCNN, and GROMACS. Under compilation, workload-specific checks, and positive speedup, 372 hotspots are accepted, including 330 real-world repository hotspots. RepoOMP achieves average speedups of $8.23\times$ on NPB and $8.96\times$ on BOTS. For the nine detailed real-world kernels used in matched-backbone and robustness analyses, RepoOMP reaches a cross-backbone mean of $5.25\times$, improves speedup by 18--28\%, and reduces agent-side token cost by 47--68\% relative to the unstructured Claude Code baseline. Across 330 accepted real-world hotspots, median speedup is $2.25\times$. Overall, RepoOMP provides an evidence-guided workflow for hotspot parallelization in repository settings. The open-source repository is available at https://github.com/Qlalq/RepoOMP_Simplified.

</details>


### [105] [Operating Multi-Node Full Fine-Tuning on NVIDIA B300: A Field Report on Telemetry-Based Triage, Negative Results, and Operational Hardening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05944)
*Seon Ho Kim,Ui Jeong Jeon,Su Hyeon Kim,Min Tae Hwang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文报告了在16块NVIDIA B300（两节点，FSDP/ZeRO-3）上全量微调32.76B参数稠密模型（Qwen3-32B）的实战运营经验，提供了四项实用工具：功率消耗分级表、诚实的负面实验结果、校准的扩展性数据，以及NCCL死锁故障排查案例。核心结论是运营层面的经验而非算法创新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B300作为新型加速器，缺乏公开的实战微调经验。作者希望通过分享在真实硬件上的集成运营经验，填补这一空白，并纠正大规模分布式训练中的常见优化误区，帮助从业者避免同类故障和时间成本损失。

Method: 不声称新算法，采用已成熟的机制（FSDP/ZeRO-3）。具体方法包括：(1) 通过板卡功耗建立诊断分类表以区分各种瓶颈；(2) 设计受控A/B实验对比NFS读取与预分词本地缓存，并重建此前"吞吐量崩溃"根因；(3) 在4/8/16 GPU配置下进行强扩展性基准测试；(4) 针对epoch结束时的NCCL死锁，设计2.7秒的预运行不变量检查门和外部监视器。

Result: 主要结果：NFS按步读取与本地缓存速度相当（约53k tokens/s），因为语料落在页缓存中且任务为计算密集型；此前"吞吐量崩溃"实为NFS/CPU争用而非存储介质限制；扩展性近似线性；通过不变量门和外部监视器将数小时静默失败转为即时拒绝，显著节省GPU小时。

Conclusion: 核心可迁移经验是运营层面的：对于数据相关的数据并行任务，应关注板卡功率而非利用率（利用率在NCCL挂起时仍显示100%）；启动前必须验证不变量，通过的冒烟测试不能证明全流程安全。

Abstract: We report operational experience full-fine-tuning a 32.76B-parameter dense model (Qwen3-32B) on 16 x NVIDIA B300 (two nodes, FSDP / ZeRO-3) -- among the first published field accounts on this accelerator. We claim no new algorithm. The individual mechanisms we use are established practice; our contribution is the integrated field experience and a set of calibrated measurements on new hardware. Concretely we offer four practitioner artifacts. (1) A B300-calibrated power-draw triage table that distinguishes compute / communication / data-starvation / checkpoint-or-deadlock / idle by board wattage (utilization% reads 100% during an NCCL hang). (2) A set of honest negative results that dispel common optimization folklore at this scale: a controlled A/B in which per-step NFS reading matches a pretokenized local cache (~53k tok/s) because the corpus fits in page cache and the job is compute-bound; and a reconstruction of an earlier "throughput collapse" as NFS/CPU contention rather than a storage-medium limit. (3) Calibrated 4/8/16-GPU strong-scaling and GPU-hour numbers on B300 (near-linear, as expected in this regime; we report absolute values as reference data). (4) A worked failure case -- an epoch-end NCCL deadlock from per-rank token-packing imbalance -- together with a 2.7-second pre-run invariant gate and an external watcher that turn multi-hour silent failures into instant rejections. This deadlock and its remedy correspond to PyTorch's documented Join / equalize-to-minimum practice; we position our instantiation against that prior art and report the GPU-hours the failure cost and the gate saves. The transferable takeaway is operational, not algorithmic: for data-dependent data-parallel jobs, watch power rather than utilization, and verify invariants before launch -- a passing smoke test is not evidence of a safe full run.

</details>


### [106] [TensorCast: The Missing Tensor Management Layer in Large Language Model Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06007)
*Yuhan Zhou,Yuchu Luo,Hao Nie,Wangrunze Lv,Yu Zhou,Yibo Zhu,Daxin Jiang,Chenren Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Tensor-as-a-Service（TaaS）抽象层和TensorCast系统，将张量状态管理与计算逻辑解耦，提供可编程的生命周期原语和分布式运行时，统一管理模型权重加载、KV缓存和检查点同步等张量生命周期任务，在与vLLM和SGLang集成测试中达到与专用系统相当的性能，并在特定场景下将中位TTFT提升最多93.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基础设施中，张量管理任务（如模型权重加载、KV缓存管理、检查点同步）被深度耦合到执行引擎、网络或存储后端中，形成孤岛化的专用系统，阻碍了张量管理策略的复用与组合，难以适应不断演化的LLM工作负载。

Method: 提出Tensor-as-a-Service（TaaS）抽象层，将张量状态管理与计算逻辑解耦；设计并实现TensorCast分布式张量管理层，提供一等公民的张量抽象、可编程生命周期原语，以及将张量管理策略与执行机制分离的运行时，开发者可通过TensorCast API编写张量管理程序，透明利用分布式执行和数据移动。

Result: 将TensorCast集成到vLLM和SGLang中，在模型权重物化、权重同步、KV缓存管理和可编程请求路由等多样化张量生命周期工作负载上进行评估，性能与专用张量管理系统相当，同时支持新的跨组件优化策略；在高度并发的多轮智能体工作负载下，可编程策略将中位TTFT提升最多93.2%。

Conclusion: 张量生命周期管理是LLM基础设施中缺失的抽象层，TensorCast证明了通过解耦张量状态管理与计算逻辑，可以在不牺牲性能的前提下实现张量管理策略的复用与跨组件优化，为LLM基础设施的模块化演进提供新方向。

Abstract: Modern LLM infrastructure increasingly manages tensors not only as computation data, but also as persistent states shared across distributed components. Existing systems optimize individual tensor management tasks, such as model weight loading, KV cache management, and checkpoint synchronization, by deeply integrating task-specific mechanisms with execution engines, networks, or storage backends. However, this specialization creates isolated silos that hinder the reuse and composition of tensor management strategies across evolving LLM workloads. In this paper, we identify tensor lifecycle management as a missing abstraction layer in LLM infrastructure and propose Tensor-as-a-Service (TaaS), which decouples tensor state management from computation logic. We design and build TensorCast, a distributed tensor management layer that provides first-class tensor abstractions, programmable lifecycle primitives, and a runtime that separates tensor management policies from execution mechanisms. This enables developers to write tensor management programs using TensorCast APIs while transparently leveraging distributed execution and data movement. We integrate TensorCast with vLLM and SGLang and evaluate it across diverse tensor lifecycle workloads, including model weight materialization, weight synchronization, KV cache management, and programmable request routing. Our results show that TensorCast achieves competitive performance with specialized tensor management systems while enabling new cross-component optimization policies. A programmable policy implemented with TensorCast improves median TTFT by up to 93.2% under highly concurrent multi-turn agent workloads.

</details>


### [107] [Routing LLM Inference to the Cleanest Grid in Real Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06188)
*Aleks Bernhard,Arif Baran Yardimci*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在大规模多区域GPU测试平台上进行了碳感知推理路由的实时验证，利用边际运行排放率（MOER）信号将推理请求路由到低碳区域，在不改变硬件或重新训练模型的情况下，可将GPU可归属的运行排放降低约50.9%（相比轮询调度），并验证了这一方案作为生产路由器的严格且可逆覆盖层的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理是快速增长的电量负载，其边际碳强度在不同电网区域和一天中不同时段差异超过一个数量级。请求放置（request placement）成为有吸引力的减碳杠杆，因为它不需要重新训练模型或更改硬件。已有研究缺乏真实生产环境的验证以及与生产路由器的对比。

Method: 在多区域GPU测试平台上，使用边际运行排放率（MOER）信号驱动碳感知推理路由。三个关键特性：1）盲基线是实际生产环境中的基于压力的路由器（而非均匀放置）；2）每个请求的能耗通过GPU遥测（NVIDIA DCGM）测量的并发曲线来归因，而非使用标称TDP；3）每个请求的碳排放结算基于历史MOER，而非仅基于驱动决策的预测。此外，回放了一整年的每小时MOER数据来量化碳感知放置的效果。

Result: 实时验证表明MOER信号可以跨区域引导推理且无调度失败，作为生产路由器的严格可逆覆盖层。年度回放显示，碳感知放置在主要配置下比轮询调度减少50.9%的GPU可归属运行排放（95%置信区间48.5-53.3%）。在会话固定之前，每小时最低MOER路由比静态年度平均MOER策略贡献约22.4个百分点（约占放置减少总量的40%）。实践观察表明，区域比较时应按绝对MOER而非百分位信号指数排名。

Conclusion: 碳感知推理路由在实际生产环境中是可行的，能够在不改变硬件或模型的情况下显著降低碳排放。由于回放使用历史MOER而非预测值，结果代表该配置下的上限估计，实际节省会因预测误差而降低。这些结果是针对单一机队和历史年份的建模结果，并非普适估计。

Abstract: Large-language-model inference is a fast-growing electricity load whose marginal carbon intensity varies by more than an order of magnitude across grid regions and across the day, making request placement an attractive lever: no retraining, no hardware change. We report a live validation of carbon-aware inference routing on multi-region GPU testbeds driven by marginal operating emissions rate (MOER) signals, with three properties uncommon in prior work: a blind baseline that is an actual production pressure-based router rather than uniform placement; per-request energy attributed from GPU telemetry (NVIDIA DCGM) via measured concurrency curves rather than nameplate TDP; and carbon settlement of every request against historical MOER, not only the forecast that drove the decision. The central live result is feasibility: a MOER signal steered inference across regions with no observed dispatch failures, as a strict and reversible overlay on the production router. To size the effect, we replay a year of hourly MOER across a grid-diverse CONUS fleet. In the primary modeled configuration, carbon-aware placement reduces modeled GPU-attributable operational emissions by 50.9% versus round-robin (95% block-bootstrap CI 48.5-53.3%). Because the replay dispatches against historical MOER rather than a forecast, this is an upper bound under that configuration; forecast error would reduce operationally realized savings. Before session pinning, hourly lowest-MOER routing contributes about 22.4 percentage points, roughly 40% of the 54.0% placement reduction, beyond a static annual-mean-MOER policy. These are modeled results for one fleet and historical year, not a universal estimate. We also record a practical observation: when comparing regions, rank by absolute MOER rather than the percentile signal-index, which is normalized within each region and answers a temporal, not a spatial, question.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [BioMedJImpact: A Comprehensive Dataset and LLM Pipeline for AI Engagement and Scientific Impact Analysis of Biomedical Journals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05227)
*Ruiyu Wang,Yuzhang Xie,Xiao Hu,Carl Yang,Jiaying Lu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了BioMedJImpact，一个基于174万篇PubMed Central文章、覆盖2744种期刊的大型生物医学导向数据集。该数据集整合了文献计量指标、合作特征和基于LLM的AI参与率，并通过分析发现作者团队规模与引用影响力正相关，而AI参与率仅在2019子集中与影响因子正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资源很少捕捉合作与AI研究如何共同塑造生物医学期刊声望，缺乏大规模、面向生物医学领域的综合数据集来衡量期刊影响力。

Method: 构建包含文献计量指标、合作特征和LLM派生的AI参与率的数据集；采用可复现的三阶段LLM流水线提取AI参与率；分两个时间子集（2016-2019, 2020-2023）分析合作强度与AI参与率对科学影响的联合作用；通过人工评估验证LLM流水线。

Result: 发现两个主要模式：作者团队规模更大的期刊往往具有更高的引用影响力；AI参与率仅在2019子集中与影响因子正相关。人工评估确认了AI相关性检测的实质性一致性和一致的子领域分类。

Conclusion: BioMedJImpact提供了生物医学与AI交叉领域的综合数据集和可扩展的、内容感知的科学计量分析框架，已在GitHub上公开代码和数据集。

Abstract: Assessing journal impact is central to scholarly communication, yet existing resources rarely capture how collaboration and artificial intelligence (AI) research jointly shape venue prestige in biomedicine. We present BioMedJImpact, a large-scale, biomedical-oriented dataset built from 1.74 million PubMed Central articles across 2,744 journals. BioMedJImpact integrates bibliometric indicators, collaboration features, and an LLM-derived AI engagement rate, defined as the proportion of AI-related articles within each journal-year. Specifically, AI engagement rate is extracted through a reproducible three-stage LLM pipeline. We analyze how collaboration intensity and AI engagement rate jointly influence scientific impact across two temporal subsets (2016-2019, 2020-2023). Two main patterns emerge: journals with larger author teams tend to have higher citation impact, while AI engagement rate is positively associated with Impact Factor only in the 2019 subset. To validate the LLM pipeline for deriving the AI engagement rate, we conduct human evaluation, confirming substantial agreement in AI relevance detection and consistent subfield classification. Together, BioMedJImpact provides both a comprehensive dataset at the interface of biomedicine and AI and a validated framework for scalable, content-aware scientometric analysis. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/JonathanWry/BioMedJImpact.

</details>


### [109] [From Trajectories to Evidence: Auditable Experimental Records for Industrial Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05235)
*Zijie Zhuang,Changxin Lao,Pengbo Xu,Hanwen Xu,Ruochen Yang,Yingzhi He,Peng Zhang,Jiangxia Cao,Yusheng Huang,Guohong Mu,Jian Liang,Ruiming Tang,Shuang Yang,Zhaojie Liu,Wenwu Ou,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究推荐系统场景中多轮机器学习实验轨迹向证据转换的问题，提出一个证据驱动框架，将工件的有界验证与执行后的声明限定相结合，确保研究过程产出的声明可审计、可追溯，并通过混合LLM控制器进行应用、延迟或拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已完成的研究轨迹并不能自动等同于有效证据：生成的工件可能不被支持或不完整，执行轮次可能无效或被混淆，后续修改可能掩盖早期发现。因此需要系统性地将轨迹转换为可验证的证据。

Method: 提出轨迹到证据转换框架：(1) 上下文隔离的生成-验证-修复流程，在发布前检查工件是否有证据违规和缺失下游需求；(2) 执行后有效性和归因检查，跨轮次合并证据；(3) 将干预级声明限定为可操作修复、诊断防护或暂扣发现；(4) 混合LLM辅助控制器基于目标证据应用、延迟或拒绝记录。

Result: 记录审计显示哪些声明通过限定；下游诊断发现肯定性适用判断是控制器瓶颈。论文到目标适应中，后期轮次常优于首轮，但最终轮次常常逊于早期最佳轮次，暴露轨迹演化的非单调性。完整流程产出的候选相对部署基线取得正向在线提升。

Conclusion: 轨迹到证据转换是研究agent在工业推荐设置中可靠运作的关键环节，所提出的证据驱动框架能有效提升声明质量、支持可审计性，并带来正向在线收益。

Abstract: Research agents increasingly conduct multi-round machine-learning experiments in industrial recommendation settings and retain the resulting trajectories to guide later decisions. Yet a completed trajectory is not automatically evidence: generated artifacts may be unsupported or incomplete, executed rounds may be invalid or confounded, and later modifications may obscure earlier findings. We study \textbf{trajectory-to-evidence conversion}, asking what a completed research process has actually established. We introduce an evidence-grounded framework that couples bounded verification of consequential artifacts with post-execution claim qualification. A context-isolated generate--verify--repair process checks artifacts for evidence violations and missing downstream requirements before release. After execution, validity and attribution checks consolidate evidence across rounds, qualify intervention-level claims as actionable repairs, diagnostic guards, or withheld findings, and preserve admitted claims as auditable records with explicit provenance and applicability boundaries. A hybrid LLM-assisted controller subsequently applies, defers, or rejects records based on available target evidence. Record audits characterize which claims survive qualification, while downstream diagnostics identify affirmative applicability judgment as a bottleneck for the tested controller. Across paper-to-target adaptations, later rounds often improve on the first, while final rounds frequently underperform an earlier best, exposing non-monotonic trajectory evolution. Candidates produced through the complete workflow also yielded positive online lifts relative to deployed baselines.

</details>


### [110] [Cross-platform epistemic verification for improving factual reliability in AI-generated news summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05302)
*Zhuo Xie,Haoze Ni*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了多源证据共识验证框架（MECV），通过聚合多个异构证据来源（源文档、维基百科、开放网络检索）和多LLM陪审团机制进行矛盾感知共识评分，对AI生成的新闻摘要进行事后幻觉纠正，在SummEdits基准上验证了其能提升事实一致性同时保留语义结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成的新闻摘要容易出现幻觉（hallucination）问题，而现有的纠正方法往往依赖单一检索通道，缺乏对多源证据的交叉验证，导致事实可靠性不足，尤其在金融新闻等敏感信息领域。

Method: 提出MECV框架，主要包括三个核心组件：(1) 从源文档、维基百科和开放网络检索中聚合多源异构证据；(2) 引入多LLM陪审团机制（使用GPT-4o-mini和DeepSeek-Chat作为验证者，Qwen-Plus作为编排者），通过矛盾感知共识评分估计事实可靠性；(3) 对识别为无证据支持的声明进行迭代最小编辑优化。

Result: 在SummEdits基准上使用GPT-4o-mini和DeepSeek-Chat作为验证陪审团、Qwen-Plus作为编排者的实验结果表明，MECV在提升事实一致性的同时保留了原始摘要的语义结构。此外，研究发现异构证据源之间的一致性可以作为识别AI生成摘要中事实不确定性的有效信号。

Conclusion: 本研究为可信AI和自动化新闻领域贡献了一个多源验证的幻觉纠正框架，证明了基于共识的验证方法能有效提升AI生成新闻摘要的事实可靠性，并展示了跨异构证据源的共识信号在识别事实不确定性方面的价值。

Abstract: This study proposes Multi-source Evidence Consen- sus Verification (MECV), a post-hoc hallucination cor- rection framework for AI-generated news summariza- tion. Instead of depending on a single retrieval channel, MECV aggregates evidence from multiple heterogeneous sources, including the source document, Wikipedia, and open-web retrieval. The framework further incorporates a multi-LLM jury mechanism that estimates factual reliabil- ity through contradiction-aware consensus scoring across verifier models. Claims identified as potentially unsup- ported are revised through iterative minimal-edit refine- ment. The proposed framework is evaluated on the SummEd- its benchmark using GPT-4o-mini and DeepSeek-Chat as the verifier jury, with Qwen-Plus as the orchestra- tor. Experimental results show that MECV improves fac- tual consistency while preserving the semantic structure of the original summaries. The findings further suggest that agreement across heterogeneous evidence sources can serve as a useful signal for identifying factual uncertainty in AI-generated summaries, including in information- sensitive domains such as financial news aggregation. This study contributes to research on trustworthy AI and automated journalism by introducing a multi-source verification framework for hallucination correction and demonstrating the value of consensus-based verification for improving factual reliability in AI-generated news summarization.

</details>


### [111] [Robustness and User-Perceived Value of Popularity Calibration in Music Recommendation: A User Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05402)
*Oleg Lesota,Gustavo Escobedo,Bruce Ferwerda,Simone Kopeinik,Dominik Kowald,Elisabeth Lex,Markus Schedl*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控实验探讨音乐推荐中流行度校准的用户感知价值和测量可靠性，发现用户能感知列表流行度构成的差异但并无明确偏好，且JSD校准指标与用户感知的相关性受多种因素影响，计算流行度标签与用户判断仅有弱关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统流行度校准研究主要依赖离线指标评估，但缺乏充分的用户研究验证，且校准推荐对用户体验的实际影响尚不明确，校准指标与用户感知之间的稳健关系在不同条件下也未得到充分考察。

Method: 从用户近期收听历史构建个性化曲目列表，使用受控朴素推荐器生成三种流行度构成的列表（高流行、低流行、校准），通过用户研究考察用户是否能感知差异、是否偏好校准列表、JSD校准在不同熟悉度和历史可用性条件下的稳健性，以及计算流行度标签与用户主观判断的一致性。

Result: 用户能够感知流行度构成的差异，但并未明确偏好校准列表；JSD与用户感知流行度之间的关系依赖于项目熟悉度、列表构成和用户历史可用性；计算流行度标签与用户判断的流行度仅存在弱对齐。

Conclusion: 研究结果促进了对流行度校准作为离线指标和面向用户的概念的更批判性理解，表明现有校准指标在实际用户体验层面可能缺乏充分的预测效度和稳健性。

Abstract: Popularity calibration in recommender systems has been studied both as a form of user-centered personalization and as an indicator of popularity bias. Most existing work evaluates calibration through offline metrics, often assuming that users prefer recommendation lists whose popularity distribution matches their historical consumption profile. However, user studies on calibration remain limited, and existing findings suggest that calibrated recommendations do not necessarily have a strong effect on user experience. Moreover, although prior work has shown that calibration metrics can correlate with users' perceptions of recommendation lists, the robustness of this relation remains unclear under different levels of item familiarity and incomplete user-history information. In this work, we study the perceived value and measurement reliability of popularity calibration in music recommendation. We construct personalized track lists from users' recent listening histories and use a controlled naive recommender to create lists with different popularity compositions: highpop-heavy, lowpop-heavy, and calibrated. We investigate whether users perceive differences between these lists, whether calibrated lists are preferred, how robust JSD-based popularity calibration is under different familiarity and history-availability conditions, and how computational popularity labels align with users' own popularity judgments. Our results show that users perceive differences in popularity composition, but do not clearly prefer calibrated lists. We further find that the relation between JSD and perceived popularity depends on item familiarity, list composition, and available user history, while computational and user-judged popularity labels only weakly align. These findings contribute to a more critical understanding of popularity calibration as both an offline metric and a user-facing construct.

</details>


### [112] [A Mechanistic Analysis of Gender Sensitivity in Dense Retrieval Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05467)
*Catherine Chen,Maarten de Rijke,Carsten Eickhoff*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过机制分析方法定位了双编码器检索模型中性别偏见的产生机制，发现性别信号源于输入嵌入并经由少量晚期注意力头传播，且不同层面的干预产生不同效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然稠密检索模型中的性别偏见已被广泛记录，但其内部产生机制尚不明确，缺乏机制层面的理解来指导有针对性的去偏见干预。

Method: 对双编码器（bi-encoder）模型进行机制性分析，定位性别敏感性的来源，从输入嵌入和晚期注意力头两个层面进行识别，并测试在这两个识别点上的引导（steering）干预效果。

Result: 发现性别信号源自输入嵌入并通过少量晚期注意力头传播，这些注意力头同时携带性别和词匹配信号；嵌入层面的引导非特异性中和了分数差异，而注意力层面的引导产生方向性偏移。

Conclusion: 研究为有针对性的去偏见提供了机制基础，同时揭示了在共享模型组件中分离性别信号与相关性信号的挑战。

Abstract: While gender bias in dense retrieval models is well documented, with prior work showing that models often score male-gendered documents higher than female or neutral variants, the internal mechanisms producing these disparities are poorly understood. In this paper, we mechanistically analyze bi-encoder models to localize gender sensitivity, finding that the signal originates in input embeddings and propagates through a small set of late-layer attention heads that carry both gender and term-matching signals. Guided by these findings, we test steering interventions at both identified points and find distinct effects: embedding-level steering non-specifically neutralizes score differences, while attention-level steering produces directional shifts. Our findings provide a mechanistic basis for targeted debiasing and highlight the challenge of disentangling gender from relevance signals in shared model components.

</details>


### [113] [An Ontology-Based Framework for Student Profiling and Content Personalization in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05489)
*José Luiz M. Morais,Arlindo F. da Conceição,Cacilda Encarnação Augusto Alvarenga,Daniela Musa*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过定性文献研究，探讨了高等教育远程课程中自主学习和在线学习实施与改进的关键因素，并提出使用本体论存储教育过程信息及相应的计算架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字信息通信技术的普及和远程/半远程教育的兴起改变了教学过程，要求学生更具主体性。研究旨在识别自主学习和在线学习在高等教育中实施与改进的重要方面，以重新思考远程课程的教学模式，使其更好地达成学习目标。

Method: 采用定性、文献综述性质的研究方法，讨论电子化、自动化地监控和记录学习过程与结果的技术，强调存储和管理学生画像（包括内容和访问形式）的重要性，并提出使用本体论存储教育过程信息，同时设计一个计算架构。

Result: 研究突出了学生画像存储与管理流程的重要性，提出了利用本体论来存储教育过程信息的方案，并给出了相应的计算架构设计。

Conclusion: 远程教育需要重新设计教学模式，通过本体论和计算架构来自动化监控和记录学习过程，从而更好地支持自主学习并提升远程课程的学习目标达成度。

Abstract: The expansion of access to Digital Information and Communication Technologies and the offer of distance or semi-distance education courses that make use of virtual learning environments brought changes in the teaching and learning processes, requiring that the student be even more protagonist in this process. The present study aimed to identify important aspects to be considered in the implementation and improvement of self-paced learning and e-learning in higher education courses, with the purpose of rethinking pedagogical models of courses offered at a distance so that they reach even more of your learning objectives. The research is characterized as qualitative, of bibliographic nature, and discusses techniques to monitor and record, electronically and automatically, the results of the process and learning. The importance of processes that store and manage the student's profile is highlighted, both in terms of content and forms of access. The article proposes the use of ontologies to store information about the educational process and presents a computational architecture for this purpose.

</details>


### [114] [EXCISE: Query-Side Exclusion for Late-Interaction Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05497)
*Mohammed Ali,Abdelrahman Abdallah,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出EXCISE方法，解决late-interaction检索器在排除查询（"包含X但不含Z"）中出现的"排除反转"问题，通过两个轻量查询端模块（仅1.5M参数）在查询时识别被排除主题并重新嵌入100篇文档的候选列表，再用无参数的规则降级匹配该主题的文档。在六个数据集和三个骨干模型上全面超越基线，并发布了分层基准X-BENCH。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Late-interaction检索器（如ColBERT）在处理排除查询时表现不佳。当用户要求包含X但排除Z时，加性MaxSim评分反而会推高覆盖Z的文档得分，作者将这一现象称为"排除反转"。作者证明，仅靠冻结向量的任何读法都无法恢复排除约束，因为困难在于识别被排除的主题，而这完全取决于查询本身。

Method: EXCISE采用查询时处理方案：两个查询端模块（共1.5M参数）分别负责识别被排除主题并对100篇文档的短名单进行重新嵌入；随后使用一个无参数的规则，降级任何与被排除主题匹配的候选文档。整个过程保持索引冻结不变。

Result: 在六个语料库、三个骨干模型的全部18个组合中，EXCISE是对抗同一骨干模型自身冻结和微调基线的最强系统。排除成功率@10从0.058提升至0.691，布尔NOT准确率从0.25-0.29提升至0.90-0.92。在1860个查询上，EXCISE优于所有微调的cross-encoder（这些cross-encoder均损失了no-harm nDCG@10），而EXCISE在其最强骨干模型上匹配了冻结基线的性能。

Conclusion: EXCISE证明了无需修改索引即可有效解决排除查询问题的可行性，显著提升了排除检索性能且不损害no-harm指标，并发布了包含显式、隐式和复合排除场景的分层基准X-BENCH，配有no-harm和布尔控制。

Abstract: Late-interaction retrievers handle exclusion queries poorly. When a user asks for X but not Z, the additive MaxSim score promotes documents covering Z, a problem we call exclusion inversion. We show that no readout of the frozen vectors recovers the constraint, because the difficulty lies in identifying the excluded topic, which depends on the query alone. EXCISE operates at query time and corrects the inversion while leaving the index frozen. Two query-side modules totalling 1.5M parameters identify the topic and re-embed a 100-document shortlist, and a parameter-free rule demotes candidates matching that topic. Across six collections and three backbones, EXCISE is the strongest system in all eighteen backbone-collection cells against that backbone's own frozen and fine-tuned baselines. It raises exclusion success@10 on ExcluIR from 0.058 to 0.691 and raises Boolean NOT accuracy from 0.25-0.29 to 0.90-0.92. Pooled over 1,860 queries, it outperforms every fine-tuned cross-encoder, each of which loses no-harm nDCG@10, whereas EXCISE matches its frozen baseline on its strongest backbone. We release X-BENCH, a tiered benchmark of explicit, implicit, and compound exclusions with no-harm and Boolean controls.

</details>


### [115] [omni-macos: On-Device Omni-Modal Search on Apple Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05543)
*Han Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 omni-macos，一个完全在 Mac 本地运行的统一多模态搜索引擎，将编码器、索引和存储全部部署在本地设备上，确保文件、查询和向量数据不出设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态搜索引擎几乎都假设服务端架构，需要将数据和查询发送到服务器处理，这带来了隐私和延迟问题。作者希望构建一个完全本地化的方案，利用 Mac 已有的统一内存架构，实现隐私保护的同时保持高效的搜索体验。

Method: 该系统的关键方法包括：(1) 在用户设定的内存预算内同时运行后台索引器和交互式搜索框；(2) 仅对编辑发生变化的块进行重新编码；(3) 用户输入时给 GPU 分配更小的计算单元；(4) 使用量化副本回答查询并进行精确重排序；(5) 将内存预算传播到统一内存上的分配器。

Result: 系统在五台 Mac 上进行了测量，覆盖了 8 倍加速器宽度范围和 32 倍内存范围，每台设备都索引各自的本地文件。

Conclusion: omni-macos 证明了在不依赖服务器的前提下，在 Mac 本地运行完整的统一多模态搜索引擎是可行的，并能有效管理内存预算。

Abstract: A search engine that embeds text, code, documents, images, audio and video into the same representation space has to run its encoder and keep its index somewhere, and almost every component built for the purpose assumes a server. We present omni-macos, which runs that whole engine, encoder, index and store, on the Mac the files are already on, so no file, query or vector ever leaves the machine. It keeps a background indexer and an interactive search box inside one memory budget the user sets: it re-encodes only the chunks an edit changes, hands the GPU smaller units while the user is typing, answers queries from a quantized replica with exact rescoring, and propagates that budget to the allocators that draw on unified memory. We measure every mechanism on five Macs spanning an eightfold range of accelerator width and a thirty-twofold range of memory, each one indexing its own local files.

</details>


### [116] [Is Personalized Modality Weighting Actually Personalized? A Controlled Audit of Per-User Weighting Claims in Multimodal Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05655)
*Jingyuan Zheng,Xin Zhang,Yang Gu,Dongjing Wang,Yuxiang Wang,Xudong Shen,Haiping Zhang,Youhuizi Li,Dongjin Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对多模态推荐系统中按用户模态加权的方法进行了大规模审计，发现简单的全局模态权重即可获得几乎所有内容增益，按用户个性化加权并未带来一致的效用提升，现有评估中常用的扰动对照（shuffle control）不够充分，需配合全局对照组（real-GM）作为个性化声明的最低证据标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献声称按用户模态偏好进行加权能提升推荐效果，但缺乏严格评估来区分真正用户特异信号与全局模态权重＋模型容量的贡献，因此有必要对此类方法进行系统性审计。

Method: 提出两对照审计原则：将六种实现统一到共享协同骨干上，测量与单一全局模态权重的效用差距（real-GM）以及与评估时置换用户权重绑定的可辨识性差距（real-shuf）；在三个短视频语料库上实验，并通过单调信号植入剂量-反应验证检测能力，同时用第四个电商语料库验证可复现性。

Result: 全局单一权重贡献了几乎全部内容增益（+1.9/+3.6/+3.5pp，p<0.001）；按用户加权无一致效用，正面收益小（≤0.9pp）且不稳定；real-shuf可达内容增益的128%但对应头同时输给全局权重，表明扰动对照不足；解耦门输入后膨胀的real-shuf归零，效用结论不变；信号植入验证显示检测能力良好（AUROC从0.57升到0.89，0.64升到1.00）。

Conclusion: 按用户模态加权方法在此前的评估中被夸大，全局模态权重可能已足够；应将real-GM与real-shuf一并报告作为个性化声明的最低证据标准，以防止虚假个性化收益的宣称。

Abstract: Per-user modality weighting is deployed at billion-user scale in multimodal recommenders, through user modality-strength vectors, attention gates, meta-weight hypernetworks, and low-rank guided weights, each claiming a ranking gain from user-specific modality preference. Yet, to our knowledge, prior evaluations do not isolate a genuinely user-specific signal from a global modality weight plus model capacity. We audit this family with a two-contrast audit principle, reducing six implementations onto one shared collaborative backbone and measuring a utility gap (real-GM) against a single global modality weight and an identifiability gap (real-shuf) against an eval-time permutation of the user-weight binding. Across three independent short-video corpora, a single global weight already delivers nearly all of the content gain (+1.9/+3.6/+3.5pp over a no-modality baseline, p < .001). Making the weight per-user adds no consistent utility: no implementation wins on all corpora and metrics, and the few positive gaps are small (<=0.9pp) and flip. The shuffle control is necessary but not sufficient, since real-shuf reaches +128% of the content gain for heads that simultaneously lose to the global weight. We trace this dissociation to gates reading the shared collaborative embedding: decoupling the gate input collapses the inflated real-shuf to near zero while the utility conclusion stands. A monotone signal-implant dose-response (capture AUROC rising from 0.57 to 0.89 and from 0.64 to 1.00) verifies the harness would detect user-specific structure if present, and every finding replicates on a fourth, cross-domain e-commerce corpus. We propose reporting real-GM alongside real-shuf as a minimum evidentiary standard for personalization claims.

</details>


### [117] [Gryphon-v2: One Model in Place of a Cascade - Generate-and-Rank Recommender with Rollout Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06213)
*Anna Lipkina,Daria Tikhonovich,Viktor Yanush,Mariia Ulianova,Oleg Sorokin,Vladislav Dodonov,Ilya Murzin,Denis Burshtein,Nikolay Savushkin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Gryphon-v2，一个统一的生成-排序端到端推荐架构。该模型对用户历史仅编码一次，通过自回归解码器生成Semantic-ID候选，并在共享编码器状态上复用排序模块。通过Rollout Distillation，将高容量Teacher Ranker的排序知识蒸馏到排序模块中，无需在服务路径中增加第二个模型。在Yandex Music的大规模在线A/B实验中，单个Gryphon-v2模型替代了包含15+候选生成器、预排序和最终排序的生产级级联系统，活跃用户数增加1.41%，且服务延迟与生产级联相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统通常采用多阶段级联架构（候选生成器、预排序、最终排序），这种架构需要重复处理用户历史、复杂特征管线和多个服务阶段。虽然基于Semantic-ID的生成式检索提供了更简单的端到端方案，但仅做下一项预测无法捕捉生产排序目标中的细粒度用户偏好。作者旨在构建一个既能简化架构又能保留生产级排序质量的统一端到端推荐系统。

Method: Gryphon-v2采用统一生成-排序架构：1）对用户历史仅编码一次；2）用自回归解码器生成Semantic-ID候选；3）将候选解析为目录物品；4）通过复用共享编码器状态的物品级排序模块进行排序。训练采用Rollout Distillation方法——Teacher Ranker的分数是唯一的排序监督信号，并在两类互补的候选分布上收集：当前解码器的rollout（暴露排序模块于服务时相同的候选生成机制）和记录的用户曝光印象（覆盖用户实际看到的物品）。Teacher Ranker仅用于训练，不在服务路径中。

Result: 在Yandex Music的大规模推荐面上，单个Gryphon-v2模型成功替代了由15+候选生成器、预排序和最终排序组成的生产级联系统。在线A/B实验显示，活跃用户数提升了1.41%，且服务延迟与生产级联相当，验证了该架构的实际工业可行性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，将生成式检索器与从Teacher Ranker蒸馏得到的排序模块相结合，可以作为生产级联系统的端到端替代方案，在工业环境中具备实际部署可行性。Gryphon-v2证明了统一的生成-排序架构能够在简化系统复杂度的同时保留细粒度的生产排序偏好。

Abstract: Industrial recommender systems are commonly deployed as multi-stage cascades with separate candidate generators, pre-rankers, and final rankers. Although effective, these cascades require repeated user-history processing, complex feature pipelines, and multiple serving stages. Semantic-ID-based generative retrieval offers a path toward simpler end-to-end systems, but next-item prediction alone does not capture the fine-grained preferences encoded by production ranking objectives. We present Gryphon-v2, a unified generate-and-rank architecture for end-to-end recommendation. The model encodes a user history once, generates Semantic-ID candidates with an autoregressive decoder, resolves them to catalogue items, and ranks them with an item-level Ranking Module that reuses the shared encoder states. To transfer fine-grained production ranking preferences without adding an expensive second model to the serving path, we distill a high-capacity, training-only Teacher Ranker into the Ranking Module. Gryphon-v2 is trained with Rollout Distillation: teacher scores are the only ranking supervision, and they are collected over two complementary candidate distributions. Rollouts from the current decoder expose the Ranking Module to candidates produced by the same generation mechanism used at serving time, while logged impressions cover items users were actually shown. In an online A/B experiment on a large-scale recommendation surface at Yandex Music, a single Gryphon-v2 model replaces a production cascade comprising more than 15 candidate generators, pre-ranking, and final ranking. The deployment increases the number of active users by 1.41% at serving latency comparable to the production cascade. These results support the practical viability of a generative retriever with a Ranking Module distilled from the Teacher Ranker as an end-to-end alternative to a production cascade.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [MEC-Patch: Visible-Infrared Cross-Modal Adversarial Attack Driven by Intrinsic Material Emissivity Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05634)
*Zhixiang Huang,Xinbo Nie,Wenxuan Wang,Lu Yang,Xin Li,Xuelin Qian,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出MEC-Patch，一种基于物理规律驱动的跨模态对抗攻击框架，利用Stefan-Boltzmann定律建立可见光-红外的跨光谱映射，构建对温度变化鲁棒的对抗扰动，并采用物理约束的NSGA-II算法和动态对抗重采样策略优化Patch参数，有效欺骗最先进的多模态检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可见光-红外多模态感知系统的对抗鲁棒性评估方法存在显著局限：要么仅关注单一模态无法绕过跨模态验证，要么将红外建模简化为启发式像素强度分布，忽略了环境温度波动对对抗稳定性的影响。

Method: 1) 基于Stefan-Boltzmann定律建立物理驱动的跨光谱映射，显式关联材料发射率与热辐射；2) 利用温度变化下全局缩放但相对对比度保持不变的特性，构建温度鲁棒的对抗扰动；3) 采用物理约束的NSGA-II算法协同优化跨模态Patch参数；4) 引入动态对抗重采样（DAR）策略增强泛化能力。

Result: 实验表明MEC-Patch能有效欺骗最先进的多模态检测器，并在高保真、物理一致、多场景的仿真环境中表现出高鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究为多模态感知系统的安全性评估提供了物理规律驱动的新视角，从底层物理机制上缓解了环境敏感性对对抗攻击稳定性的影响。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of visible-infrared multimodal perception systems in safety-critical domains such as autonomous driving, evaluating their cross-modal adversarial robustness has become increasingly vital. However, existing approaches exhibit significant limitations in approximating the intrinsic laws of imaging. Most studies either focus on a single modality, failing to bypass cross-modal verification, or simplify infrared modeling into heuristic pixel-intensity distributions, neglecting the impact of ambient temperature fluctuations on adversarial stability. To bridge this gap, this paper proposes MEC-Patch, a cross-modal adversarial attack framework driven by intrinsic physical laws. By leveraging the Stefan-Boltzmann Law, we establish a physics-grounded cross-spectral mapping that explicitly links material emissivity to thermal radiation. Building on this formulation, we reveal that, under a fixed emissivity distribution, ambient temperature variations induce consistent global scaling while preserving relative emissivity-induced contrast. We exploit this property to construct temperature-robust adversarial perturbations whose discriminative patterns remain stable in the infrared modality, thereby fundamentally mitigating environmental sensitivity. Furthermore, we employ the physics-constrained NSGA-II algorithm to synergistically optimize the material-distribution-based patch parameters effective across both modalities, while enhancing generalization through a Dynamic Adversarial Resampling (DAR) strategy. Experimental results demonstrate that MEC-Patch effectively deceives state-of-the-art multimodal detectors and exhibits high robustness within high-fidelity, physically-consistent, and multi-scene simulation environments. This research provides a physical-law-driven perspective for the security assessment of multimodal perception systems.

</details>


### [119] [Whence the Voice? Self-supervised Dual-source Audio-Visual Localisation via Selective Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05816)
*Han Hu,Dongheng Lin,Yuqi Hou,Haotian Li,Hyung Jin Chang,Jianbo Jiao*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文发现自监督音频-视觉对比学习在遇到多个声源时，会自然地收敛到最显著的视听对应关系（即选择性收敛），并利用这一现象提出了一个两阶段渐进框架：先利用选择性收敛识别主导声源，再借助学到的先验发现剩余声源，从而打破"分离音频需知声源位置、定位声源需分离音频"的循环依赖。该方法在双声源基准上达到自监督方法最优，部分指标甚至超越弱监督方法。此外，作者指出现有基准存在评估不一致问题（连续热力图与边界框标注的系统性偏差），并引入像素级分割掩码以进行空间对齐评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多声源视觉场景中的声音定位面临固有的循环依赖问题：混合音频的分离需要知道声源位置，而定位声源又需要分离的音频信号。现有方法难以在无监督条件下同时解决这两个相互依赖的子任务。

Method: 提出两阶段渐进式自监督框架：(1)利用对比学习模型在多个声源场景下的"选择性收敛"现象识别主导声源；(2)利用已学习的主导声源先验来发现剩余的次要声源。同时引入像素级分割掩码评估方案以修正现有基准中热力图与边界框标注不匹配的问题。

Result: 在双声源基准上达到自监督方法最优性能，部分指标超越弱监督方法；新引入的像素级分割掩码评估方案提供了更准确的空间对齐评估。

Conclusion: 拥抱而非抑制选择性（选择性收敛）提供了一条可扩展、无需标注的多声源定位路径。该现象与人类选择性听觉注意类似，为打破视听定位中的循环依赖提供了自然且有效的思路。

Abstract: Localising multiple sound sources in visual scenes remains a fundamental challenge in multimodal perception due to an inherent circular dependency: separating mixed audio requires knowing source locations, while identifying sound-producing regions requires separated audio signals. In this paper, we focus on the dual-source setting and discover a selective convergence in self-supervised audio-visual learning: when presented with multiple sound sources, contrastive models naturally converge to the most salient audio-visual correspondence rather than attempting to represent all sources equally. This emergent phenomenon, analogous to human selective auditory attention, enables us to break the above circular dependency through a progressive two-stage framework: first, leveraging selective convergence to identify dominant sources, and then exploiting these learned priors to uncover remaining sources. Our self-supervised approach achieves the best performance among self-supervised methods on dual-source benchmarks without requiring any manual annotations, and even surpasses some weakly-supervised approaches \red{on certain metrics. Furthermore, we identify a fundamental evaluation inconsistency in existing benchmarks: comparing continuous localisation heatmaps against bounding-box annotations creates systematic biases, particularly for non-axis-aligned objects where the bounding box includes substantial background regions. To address this, we introduce pixel-level segmentation masks to the existing benchmark, enabling spatially-aligned evaluation. Together, these results suggest that embracing rather than suppressing selectivity offers a scalable, annotation-free route to multi-source localisation.

</details>


### [120] [M$^3$Prune: Hierarchical Collaborative Pruning for Efficient Multi-Modal Multi-Agent Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05967)
*Taolin Zhang,Weizi shao,Zijie Zhou,Chen Chen,Daiyang Yu,Tingyuan Hu,Chengyu Wang,Xiaofeng He*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: M3Prune是一个多模态多智能体层次通信图剪枝框架，通过消除模态内和模态间的冗余通信边来提升多模态检索增强生成（mRAG）系统的性能与token效率之间的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态检索增强生成（mRAG）多智能体系统虽然表现出色，但存在大量token开销和计算成本，难以大规模部署。如何在不牺牲性能的前提下降低通信开销是亟待解决的问题。

Method: M3Prune首先在文本和视觉模态内部进行图稀疏化，识别任务关键的通信链路；随后构建模态间通信图并通过模态对齐分数稀疏化跨模态连接，鼓励一致的跨模态推理；最后逐步剪枝冗余边以获得高效的层次拓扑结构。

Result: 在通用领域和特定领域的mRAG基准上，M3Prune一致性地优于单智能体和强多智能体mRAG系统，同时显著提升了token效率。

Conclusion: M3Prune通过层次化的通信图剪枝策略，在保持甚至提升多智能体mRAG系统性能的同时大幅降低token开销，为多模态多智能体系统的大规模部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in multi-modal retrieval-augmented generation (mRAG), which augments multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) with external knowledge, have shown that collective intelligence from multiple agents can outperform a single model through effective communication. Despite their strong performance, existing multi-agent systems incur substantial token overhead and computational cost, posing challenges for large-scale deployment. To address these issues, we propose a Multi-Modal Multi-agent hierarchical communication graph PRUNING framework, termed M3Prune. M3Prune eliminates redundant communication edges both across and within modalities, improving the trade-off between task performance and token overhead. Specifically, M3Prune first performs intra-modal graph sparsification in the textual and visual modalities to identify task-critical communication links. It then constructs an inter-modal communication graph and sparsifies cross-modal connections while encouraging consistent cross-modal reasoning through a modality alignment score. Finally, it progressively prunes redundant edges to obtain an efficient hierarchical topology. Extensive experiments on both general-domain and domain-specific mRAG benchmarks show that M3Prune consistently outperforms single-agent and strong multi-agent mRAG systems while signifi- cantly improving token efficiency.

</details>
